1a1(1): ka
1a2(1): rnams 'phrul mdo bzhugs so/
1b1(1): @#//rgya gar skad du/ar+ya many+dzu shrI bi kurbA Na pa ri brta nA ma ma hA yA na su tra/bod skad du/'phags pa 'jam dpal rnam par 'phrul ba'i le'u zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/sangs rgyas
1b2(1): dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das nyan na yod na rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra ba na dge slong stong nyis
1b3(1): brgya lnga bcu'i dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang thabs cig tu bzhugs te/byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun chen po dang/'dod pa na spyod pa dang/gzugs na spyod pa dang/gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu du ma dag dang yang thabs
1b4(1): cig go/de na bcom ldan 'das 'khor brgya stong du mas yongs su bskor cing mdun gyis bltas nas chos ston to//de'i tshe lha'i bu 'od mang po zhes bya ba 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur te 'dug go/'jam dpal gzhon nur
1b5(1): gyur pa rang 'khor de nyid du 'dus bar gyur te 'dug go/de nas lha'i bu 'od mang pos 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la 'di skad ces smras so//'jam dpal byang chub sems dpa la rnams kyi bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i
1b6(1): byang chub la bdud par chad byed na/'jam dpal byang chub semasad pa' rnams kyi bdud kyi las gang yin/de skad ces smras pa dang/'jam dpal gzhon nur rgyur pas lha'i bu 'od mang po la 'di skad ces smras so //lha'i bu
1b7(1): ji tsam du las rnams 'byung ba de tsam du bdud kyi las so//ji tsam du smon pa dang/ji tsam du log par 'dzin pa dang/ji tsam du mchog tu 'dzin pa de tsam du bdud kyi lasaso//ji tsam du 'dod pa dang/ji tsam du 'du shes pa dang/
1b8(1): ji tsam du rlom sems pa dang/ji tsam du yongs su rtog pa de tsam du bdud kyi la so//lha'i bu gzhan yang byang chub kyi sems la mngon par zhen pa'ang bdud kyi las so//sbyin pa'i sems la mngon par zhenapa'ang
1b9(1): bdud kyi las so//tshul khrims dang/bzod pa dang/brtson 'grus dang/bsam gtan dang/shes rab kyi sems la mngon par zhan pa'ang bdud kyi las so //sbyin pa rlom sems su byed pa dang/tshul khrims
2a1(1): @#//mchog tu 'dzin pa dang /bzod pa 'dzin pa dang /brtson 'grus len pa dang /bsam gtan la mtshan ma dang /shes rab rgyu ba'ang bdud kyi las so//dgon pa la dga' zhing mos pa'i sems dang /btang snyoms su ltung ba'ang bdud
2a2(1): kyi las so//'dod pa nyung ba dang /chog shes pa dang /sbyangs pa'i yon tan dang /yo byad bsnyungs pa rlom sems su byed pa'ang bdud kyi las so//bdag ni stong pa nyid la gnas pa/bdag ni mtshan ma med pa la gnas pa/bdag ni smon pa med
2a3(1): pa la gnas pa/bdag ni spros pa med pa la gnas pa/ba dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i bka' bsgrub cing gnas pa'o snyam du rlom sems su byed cing rnam par rtog pa'ang bdud kyi las so//lha'i bu rtog pa dang /rnam par rtog pa dang /yongs su
2a4(1): rtog pa dang /mthong ba dang /thos pa dang /bye brag phyed pa dang /rnam par shes pa'i gnas ji tsam pa de tsam du bdud kyi las so//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal bdud kyi las ga las 'byung /'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu bdud
2a5(1): kyi las ni brtson pa las 'byung ngo //de ci'i phyir zhe na/lha'i bu brtson pa la bdud kyi glags tshol gyi/mi brtson pa bdud kyis ci zhig bya bar 'gyur te/de nyid bdud kyi las yin no//lha'i bus smras pa/'dzam dpal byang chub sems
2a6(1): dpa'i ji ltar na brtson pa yin/ji ltar na mi brtson pa yin/'jam dpal gyis smras pa /lha'i bu ji tsam du gnyis la sbyor ba de tsam du brtson pa ma yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'jig rten gnas pa ni gnyis la sbyor ba dang ldan pa'o//lha'i bu brtson pa
2a7(1): zhes bya ba 'di ni sbyor ba yang dag pa'i tshig bla dgas so//sbyor ba yang dag pa zhes bya ba 'di ni sbyor pa med pa'i tshig bla dgas so//sbyor ba med pa zhes bya ba 'di ni spros pa med pa'i tshig bla dgasaso//spros pa med pa zhes bya ba 'di nisbyor ba yang dag pa'i tshig
2a8(1): bla dgas so//lha'i bu gang na mig la mi sbyor ba dang /gzugs la mi sbyor ba 'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i sbyor ba yang dag pa zhes ba'o//gang na rna ba la mi sbyor ba dang /sgra la mi sbyor ba dang /sna la mi sbyor ba dang /dri la mi sbyor ba
2a9(1): dang /lce la mi sbyor ba dang /ro la ma sbyor ba dang /lus la mi sbyor ba dang /reg bya la mi sbyor ba dang /yid la mi sbyor ba dang /chos la mi sbyor ba 'di ni bya chub sems dpa'i sbyor pa yang dag pa zhes bya'o//lha'i bu gzhan yang
2b1(1): nyon mongs pa thams cad la'ang mi sbyor la/sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa spong ba'i phyir yang brtson pa 'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i sbyor ba yang dag pa zhes bya'o//lha'i bu gzhan yang khams gsum pa thams cad la'ang mi spyor la/khams
2b2(1): gsum du gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyang 'byung bar sbyor ba 'di ni byang chub sems pa'i sbyor ba yang dag pa shes bya'o//gzhan yang sbyin pa la rlom sems med par yang sbyor la/sems can ser sna can sdud par yang sbyor dang /tshul
2b3(1): khrims la rlom sems med par byang sbyor la/sems can tshul khrims 'chal ba sdud par sbyor pa dang /bzod pa la rlom sems med bar yang sbyor la/sems cad gnod sems can sdud par yang sbyor ba dang /brtson 'grus la rlom sems med
2b4(1): par yang sbyor la/sems chan le lo can sdud par yang sbyor pa dang /bsam gtan la rlom sems med par gang sbyor la/sems can sems rnam par g.yengs pa sdud par yang sbyor ba dag/shes rab la rlom sems med par
2b5(1): sems can shes rab 'chal ba can sdud par yang sbyor 'di'ang byang chub sems dpa'i sbyor ba yang dag pa zhes bya'o//gzhan yang stong pa nyid la rlom sems med par 'ang sbyor la/lta bar song ba
2b6(1): thams cad dang yang bral zhing blta bar song ba las rnam par grol ba'ang ma yin pa ni stong pa nyid la sbyo ba ste/stong pa nyid gang gis stong pa nyid stong pa'i stong pa nyid des lta bar gyur pa rnams stong pa dang /stong pa nyid
2b7(1): gang gis lta bar gyur pa rnams song pa'i stong pa nyid des chos thams cad stong par 'di shes shing shes pa la lhag pa'i nga rgyal yang med pa gang yin pa 'di'ang byang chub sems pa'i sbyor ba yang dag pa'o//lha'i bu gzhan yang
2b8(1): mtshan ma med pa la rlom sems med par yang sbyor la//rnam par rtog pa thams cad dang bral zhing rnam par rtog pa las grol ba'ang ma yin pa ni
3a1(1): @#//mtshan ma myed pa la sbyor bya ste/rnam par rtog pa gang gis /mtshan ma myed pa la rnam par brtog pa'i/rnam par rtog pa de yang mtshan ma med pa dang/mnyam zhing rnam par rtog pa de dang/
3a2(1): mtshan ma myed par yang mtshan ma myed par rnam par rtog pa mnyam par shes te/'di shes shing shes pa la lhag pa'i nga rgyal yang myed pa gang yin pa 'di yang/byang chub sems dpa'i sbyor ba yang dag pa'o//gzhan yang smond pa myed
3a3(1): pa rlom sems myed par yang sbyor la/bsams bzhin du skye ba yongs su 'dzind par yang sbyor te/de smon lam 'debs kyang sems kyis bag la nyal bar yang myi byed cing/smon lam de bas kyang/smon lam gyis rgyu ba yang myed
3a4(1): la/skye yang skye zhing gza' gnyi gar lta ba /bral ba 'di lta ste/ngar 'dzind pa dang/da yir 'dzind pa'i lta ba dang bral bar gyurd cing skye yang skye la/'chi 'pho dang/skye ba dang/yang bral ba yin te 'di shes shing shes pa la
3a5(1): lhag pa'i nga rgyal yang myed gang yin pa/'di byang chub sems dpa'i sbyor pa yang dag pa'o//lha'i bu gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' pa rnams kyi sbyor pa yang dag pa yang dag phul ni/gang shes rab kyi shes
3a6(1): pas brtson par yang myed la/thabs kyi shes rab dge ba'i chos thams cad sdud par yang sbyor ba dang/shes rab kyis shes pas bdag myed pa dang/sems can myed pa dang/srog myed pa dang/bso' pa
3a7(1): myed pa dang/gang zag myed par yang sbyor la/thabs kyi shes pas sems can thams cad yongasu smyind par byed par yang sbyor ba dang /shes rab kyi shes pas chos thams cad blang ba myed pa dang/dor ba myed par yang sbyor la/
3a8(1): thabs kyi shes pas chos thams can yongasu 'dzind par yang sbyor ba dang//shes rab kyi shes pas sangs rgyas thams cad dang/chos kyi dbyings dbyer myed par yang sbyor la/thabadas kyi shes pas sangs rgyas thabs cad la/bla mar bya ba
3a9(1): dang/gus par bya ba dang/mchod pa dang/rim gro byed par yang sbyor ba dang/shos rab kyi shes pas sangs d+yas kyi zhing thams cad nam mkha'i zhing du yang sbyor la/thabs kyi she bas sangs rgyas kyi zhing gyi yon tan bkod pa yongasu
3a10(1): sbyor ba'i rgyan la yang sbyor ba dang/shes rab kyi shes pa la 'phags pa thams cad 'dus ma byas kyis rab tu 'bye' bar yang sbyor la/thabs kyi shos pas 'phags pa thams cad la bla ma dang gus pa dang/rim gro byed pa yang sbyor
3b1(1): ba dang/shes rab kyi shes bas/sangs rgyas kyi sku gzag pa myed par so sor rtog par yang sbyor la/thabs kyi shes bas/mtshan dang/dbye byad bzang po dang/rgyan la yang dbyor ba dang/shes rab kyi shes pas/
3b2(1): 'du byed thams cad ma skyes pa dang/ma byung bar so sor rtog par yang byung la/thabs kyi shes pas bsams bzhin du srid bar skye ba yongasu 'dzind par yang sbyor ba dang/shes rab kyi shas pas stong pa nyid dang/mtshan ma myed pa dang/
3b3(1): smond pa myed pa yang sbyor la/thabs kyi shas pas lta ba dang/rnam par rtog pa dang/smon lam dbang bar stond bar yang sbyor ba dang/shesrab kyi shes pas mngon bar shes pa skye ba myed bar yang sbyor la/thabs kyi shis pas mngon par
3b4(1): shes pa thams cad kyis rnam par 'phruld bar yang sbyor ba dang /shes rab kyi shes pas phung po dang/khams dang/skye mched ma skyes pa dang/myi lcogs par yang sbyor la/thabs gyi shes pas phung po dang//khams dang/skye mched
3b5(1): yongs su dor bar yang sbyor ba dang/shis rab kyi shes pas srid pa mya ngan las 'das pa'i rab bzhin du rtogs par byed par yang sbyor la/thabs kyi/shes bas 'khor ba ni sems can yongs su smyind par byed par yang sbyor ba
3b6(1): dang/shes rab kyi shes pas shes rab kyi dmyig la yang sbyor la/thabs kyi shes pas lha'i dmyig mngon bar bsgrub pa yang sbyor pa dang/shes rab kyi shes pas chos rjod du myed par khong du chud par byed par yang sbyor
3b7(1): kyi shes pas yi ge dang des pa'i tshig gis chos stond bar yang sbyor ba dang/shes rab kyi shes bas don dang chos so sor yang dag par dag par rig pa yang sbyor la/thabs kyi shas pas des pa'i tshig dang/spobs pa so sor yang dag par rig
3b8(1): pa la yang sbyor ba dang/shes rang kyi shes pas sems can gyi dbang po mchog dang/mchog ma yin pa thams cad shes pa la/yang sbyor/thabs kyi shes pas so sor ci rigs par chos stand par yang sbyor ba dang/shes ba kyi shes
3b9(1): bas sems can gyi spyod pa brgyad khri bzhi stong khong du chud par byed bar yang sbyor la/thabs kyi shis pas chos kyi phung po brgyad khri bzhi stong yang dag par stond/bar yang sbyor pa 'di ni/lha'i bu byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sbyor
3b10(1): pa yang dag par yang dag phul lo/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyurd pas/sbyor ba yang dag pa'i bstand pa na/'khor de na lha'i bu rgya ston ni/bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skyes so/
4a1(1): @##//byang chub sems dpa' lnga stong ni/myi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa skyes so//bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang/'jam dpal zho nur gyurd pa la/'jam dpal khyod kyis/byang chub sems dpa' rnams 
4a2(1): kyi sbyor ba yang dag pa 'di/legs bar bshad pa/legs so legs so/legs so zhes bya ba byind to//de nas lha'i bu 'od mang pos 'jam dpal zho nur gyurand pa la 'di skad ces smras so//'jam dpal khyod 
4a3(1): la de bzhin gshegs pa gang gi phyir legs so zhes bya ba byin bas/ci khyod dga' bar 'gyur ram/'jam dpal byis smras pa lha'i bu ci snyam du sems/gang stsald pa la legs so zhes bya ba byinan/stsald ba de la 
4a4(1): ci dga' bar 'gyur ba lta yod dang/lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal sbruld pa la rjes su chags pa yang myed/khong khro yang myed na/de la dga' bar 'gyur ba ci gzhig yod//'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu 
4a5(1): de bzhin du/chos thams cad kyang stsald pa'i ngo bo nyid kyi mtshan nyid de/de bzhin gshegs pa yang de'i tshan nyid la 'jam dpal yang dge mtshan nyid la/de la legs so zhes bya bas/kho bo dga' bar 
4a6(1): 'gyur du ci zhig yod/lha'i bu 'di lta ste dper na/brag cha ni dgra snyand pa dang/myi snyand pa rnams la rjes su chags bar yang myi 'gyur khong khro bar myi 'gyur ro//lha'i bu de bzhin du/chos thams
4a7(1): cad ni brag cha'i ngo bo nyid kyi rang bzhin te/de bzhin gshegs pa lang de'i bzhin na/de la legs so zhes ba bas kho bo dga' bar 'gyur du ci yod//lha'i bus smras pa/'jas dpal de bzhin gshegs bas su la legs so zhes bya ba 
4a8(1): gnang //'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu rnam bar shes pa myi gnas bar stond pa dang/bstand pa thams cad la su'i blo thos bar myi 'gyur pa dang/su sems can thams cad la sems can du 'du shes myi 'jug pa dang/chos 
4a9(1): thams cad la chos su 'du shes myi 'jug pa dang /su bsam ba mya ngan las 'das pa la yang gnas la/sbyor ba 'khor ba na yang gnas pa dang/su ba rung kyi las khod du chud nas/bdud kyi las kyang/sangs rgyas kyi drin las 
4a10(1): su shes shing/sangs rgyas kyi phrin las kyang bdud kyi lasu shes pa de lta bu chos dang/ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' la/lha'i bu de bzhin gshegs bas legs so zhes bya ba gnang ngo/lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal 
4b1(1): khyod ci de lta bu'i chos dang ldan bas/de bzhin gshegs pa khyod la legs so zhes bya ba gnang ngam/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu 'di ji snyam du sems/gang mnyam ba nyid de la/myi gnyam ba gang yang yod dam /lha'i
4b2(1): bus smras pa/'jam dpal mnyam ba nyid la/myi gnyam mnyam ba gang yang myed do//'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu gnyam ba nyid gang dang/mnyam ba nyid de dang/mtshan ma myed pa mnyam mo/mtshan ma myed pa gang dang
4b3(1): mnyam ba de dang/kho po mnyam mo/mnyam ba nyid gang dang/ma nyam pa de dang/lta bar 'gyur pa rnams mnyam mo//lta bar gyurd pa rnams gang dang/mnyam pa de dang/kho po mnyam ma//bdag nyid gang dang/mnyam pa dedang
4b4(1): phyin ci log rnams mnyam mo/phyin ci log rnams gang dang/mnyam ba de dang/kho bo mnyam mo/mnyam ba nying gang dang/mnyam ba dedang/dmyigs pa mnyam mo/dmyigs pa gang dang/mnyam pa de rang kho bo mnyam mo//mnyam
4b5(1): nyid gang dang mnyam ba de dang /'khor pa'i mtha' mnyam mo//'khor pa'i mtha' gang dang mnyam ba de da/mya ngan las 'das pa'i mtha' mnyam mo//ma rig pa dang/srid pa dang/sred pa'i mtha' gang dang/mnyam d+ge 
4b6(1): dang/ma rig pa rnam bar grol pa'i mtha' myed do//ma rig pa dang/rnam bar grol pa'i mtha' gang dang mnyal ba de dang/'dod chags dang zhe sdang dang/gting mug gi mtha' mnyam mo//'dod chags dang/
4b7(1): zhe sdang dang gti mug gi mtha' gang dang/mnyam ba de nga/stong pa nyid dang/mtshan ma myed pa dang/smond pa myed pa'i mtha' mnyam mo/stong pa nyid dang//mtshan ma myed pa dang/smon pa myed pa'i mtha' gang dang/mnyam ba de
4b8(1): dang/'dus byas kyi mtha' mnyam ba nyid//'dus byas kyi mtha' gang ngagmnyam ba de dang/'dus ma byas kyi mtha' mnyam mo//'dus ma byas kyi mtha' gang dang/mnyam pa de dang/'jam dpal zho nur gyurd pa mnyam
4b9(1): ste/lha'i bu mnyam pa nyid des chos thams cad dang mnyam mo//de la su chad pa dang/gang chad pa dang/gang la bshad pa dang/su la legs so zhes bya ba byind pa'i chos de dag thams cad nam mkha' dang/mnyam ba nyid kyis
4b10(1): mnyam ste/nam mkha' 'dus ma byas yin bas/nam mkha' ni mnyam ba yang myed/myi mnyam ba yang myed/rjes su myi 'gro pa'i tshul gyis mnyam ba nyid 'di dag ni rjes su song ba de la/de bzhin gshegs bas
5a1(1): @#//legs so zhes bya ba gnang ngo//lha'i bus smras pa /'jam dpal khyod legs so zhes bya ba dang/legs so zhes bya ba ma yin las brtsams te/chos bstand pa las spobs bar gyis 
5a2(1): shig//'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i bu lhag pa'i bsam bas byang chub kyi phyir 'jug na legs pa'o//g.yo' dang/rgyus nye bar spyod pa ni legs pa ma yin pa'o//snying rje chen pos sems can thams cad myi gtong 
5a3(1): pa ni legs pa'o//sems can rnams la rnam par 'tshe ba ni legs pa ma yin pa'o//sems can thams cad la zhe (ma) 'grag+sa pa'i(sa)sa ni legs pa'o//don sdems kyi bsam ba ni legs pa ma yin pa'i//nyes pa 'byung ba myi 'chab 
5a4(1): cing 'chags ste//rnam par 'byed pa la kun nas bskord pa myed bar gnas pa ni legs pa'o//nyes 'byung ba 'chab pa ni legs pa ma yin no//bdag gis 'khruld pa ni so sor rtog pa ni legs pa'o//gzhan gyi 'khrul
5a5(1): da pa'i skyon tshol ba ni legs pa ma yin ba'o//byas pa bzo' ba dang/byas pa shes pa dang/byas pa chud myi zon pa dang/byas pa la lan tu phan 'dogs pa ni legs pa'o/byams pa myi bzo' ba dang/byams
5a6(1): pa myi shes pa dang/byas pa cud myi bzon pa dang/byas pa land tu 'phan myi 'dogs pa ni legs pa ma yin pa'o/thos pa yang yang dag bar bsgrubs pa ni legs pa'o/thos pa legs ba rim bsgrub pa ni legs pa ma yin nga'o
5a7(1): //bslab pa yang dag bar blangs nas ma nyams pa ni legs pa'o//slab pa yang dag bar blang pa las 'ad ba ni legs pa ma yin pa'o//dben ba la sems gzhol zhing dben ba la dag pa ni legs pa'o//'du 'dzi la dga' ba ni legs 
5a8(1): pa ma yin pa'o//'gon pa la gnas bar lus dang/srog 'dor pa ni legs pa'o/lus dang srog la lta pa ni legs pa ma yin ba'o//'phags pa'i rigs la yang dag bar 'ga' ba ni ngo bo rnams la tshod rig pa ni legs pa'o//
5a9(1): chog myi shes shing 'dod pa che la sdig pa 'dod pa ni legs pa ma yin pa'i//nyams chung ba rnams la bzod cing dang tu lend ba ni legs pa'o//sems can rnams la sma 'bebs pa ni legs pa ma yin pa'o//skyabs su song ba rnams myi gtod 
5a10(1): ba ni legs pa'o//skyabs su song ba rnam la 'khu ba ni legs pa ni legs pa ma yin ba'o//dam bcas pa las myi nyams shing tshid bden ba/ji skad smras pa de bzhin du byed pa la legs pa'o//'jig rten thams cad la slu ba ni/
5b1(1): legs ba ma yin pa'o//dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzind pa ni legs pa'o/dam pa'i chos spangs pa ni legs pa ma yin pa'o//blos pa gser myed cing/chos la slob spon gyi dpe' khyud myed do/zang zing myed bar chos
5b2(1): bston pa ni legs pa'o//chos la gser sna byed cing chos la slob dpon gyi dbe' mkhyud byed de//zang zing dang 'breld pa'i sems kyis chos bstond pa ni legs pa ma yin ba'o//thos pa la ser sna myed cing/sems can yongs su smyin bar
5b3(1): byed pa'i thabs dang ldan ba'i sdud ba legs pa'o//sems can la myi lta zhing thabs dang myi ldan bar sdug pa'i dngos po bstond pa ni legs pa mayin pa'o//pha rold tu phyind pa drug gi tshol ba ni legs pa'o//pha rold tu phyin pa drug
5b4(1): gi mi mthun pa'i phyogs ni legs pa ma yin pa'o//shes pas bya ba'i las rnams ni legs pa'o//da rgyal gyis byas pa'i las rnams ma yin ba'o//nga rgyal gyis byas pa'i las ma ni legs pa ma yin pa'o/brda'
5b5(1): pa bstand pa ni legs pa'o//ngan pa dang shes pa ni legs pa ma yin pa'o/ /dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam la rtend pa ni legs pa'o//myi dge' ba bcu'i las kyi lam du zhugs pa ma yin pa'o//bsdig
5b6(1): pa'i las thams cad myi spyod pa ni legs pa'o//bsdig pa'i las thams cad la zhugs pa ni legs pa ma yin ba'o/lha'i bu myigs pa can gyi rab tu 'byung ni legs pa ma yin pa'o //stong pa nyid 'byung
5b7(1): pa'i mngon bar 'byung pa'i legs pa'o//'jig rten kyang phan pa'i rnam smras pa'i seng ge'i khri ni legs pa ma yin ba'o //byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod rab tu thob pa'i dam pa'i chos smras pa'i seng ge'i khri ni logs pa'o//tshul khrims
5b8(1): tshald pas dang bas sbyin pa'i spyod pa ni legs pa ma yin pa'i//tshul khrims dang ldan pa ni thob pa dang/bkur sdi dang/tshigs su bcad pa thams cad ni legs pa'o//lhag pa'i nga rgyal can gyi byang chub ni legs pa ma
5b9(1): yin pa'o//lhag pa'i nga nga rgyal myed bar shes pa brda' sprod pa ni legaso/byang chub sems dpa' rnams la gnod bar sems dpa' ni legs pa ma yin pa'o//byang chub sems dpa' rnam la ston dpa tsam du 'phangs bar 'dzind
6a1(1): @#//pa ni legs pa'o//byang chub sems dpa'i mying gis rnyes bas 'tsho ba ni legs pa ma yin pa'o//byang chub sems pa'i chos yang dag bar sgrub pa las gnas pa'i rnyed pa ni//lha'i bu de ltar na ci tsam du
6a2(1): de bzhin gshegs pa'i bka dang//'gal ba de btsam du thams cad legs pa ma yin pa'o//ci tsam du de bzhin gshegs pa'i bkad//thun ba de tsam de thams cad legs pa'o//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam
6a3(1): dpal khyod ni legs so zhes bya ba pa'i chos la brtson nam//'on te legs ma yin zhes bya pa'i chos la brtson/'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i bu kho po ni legs so zhes bya pa'i chos la brtson ba yang ma yin//legs
6a4(1): pa yang ma yin zhes bya ba'i chos la brtson ba yang mayin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/lha'i bu kho po ni chos thams cad la brtson ba ma yin no//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal khyod ci dge' ba'i chos la myi brtson nam/
6a5(1): 'jam dpal gis smras pa lha'i bu kho po ni dge pa'i chos la brtson ba yangam yin//myi dge pa'i chos la brtson pa yang ma yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na /lha'i bu dge ba zhes bya ba 'di yang 'dus byas so
6a6(1): //myi dge ba zhes bya ba 'di 'dus byas so//myi g.yo' ba zhes bya ba'i yang 'dus byas so//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam pa dpal khyod ci 'dus ma byas pa 'am/'jam dpal gis smras pa /lha'i bus
6a7(1): gal te 'dus byas kyis skrag pa lta na ni/kho po 'dus ma byas su 'gyur ro//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam ma dpal khyod ci/'dus byas kyis myi skrag gam/'jam dpal gis smras ba /lha'i bu gal te yang dag bar 'dus byas kyi
6a8(1): yongs su grub pa zhig mthong na ni/kho po 'dus byas kyis skrag bar 'gyur ro//lha'i bus smrasapa/'jam dpal ci khyod kyis yang dag bar 'dus byas kyang yongs su grub pa ma mthong dam/'jam dpal gis smras sa/lha'i bu kho pos ni yang
6a9(1): dag bar 'dus byas kyi/yongs su grub pa ma mthod/'dus ma byas kyi yang ma yin no//lha'i bus smras pa/'jamadapal khyod ci 'dus byas su rtogs bsam/'on te 'dus ma byas su rtogs sam 'jam gyis smras pa pa/
6a10(1): lha'i bu kho po ni 'dus ma byas su gtogs pa yang ma yin/'dus ma byas su gtogs yang ma yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/gal te 'dus byas su rtogs bar na ni/byis pa so so'i skye'o thams cad dang 'dra bar 'gyur ro//
6b1(1): gal te 'dus ma byas su gtogs par gyurd na ni nyan thos pa dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad 'dra bar 'gyur ro//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal gal te khyod/'dus byas su rtogs pa yang ma yin/'dus ma byas su rtogs pa
6b2(1): yang ma yin na/khyod ci ltar shes par bya/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu 'di ci snyam du sems/gang de bzhin gshegs pa sruld pa 'dus byas su gartogs sam/'on te 'dus ma byas su gartogs/lha'i bus smras
6b3(1): pa/'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs bas sruld pa ni/'dus byas su rtogs pa lang ma yin/'dus ma byas so rtogs pa yang ma yin no/'jam dpal gyis smras pa /lha'i bu de bzhin shegs pas sruld pa'i chos kyi de bzhin
6b4(1): nyid ji 'dra ba de 'dra bar kho bo shes par gyis shig/lha'i bu 'di ci bsnyam du sems/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam bar shes pa gang la gnas/gzugs la gnas sam/smras pa ma yin no/smras pa ci tshor ba dang/'du shes
6b5(1): dang/'du byed rnams la gnas sam/smras pa la yin no/smras pa de ci 'dus byas la gnas sam/smras pa mi yin no/smras pa de/ci'i/'dus byas la gnas sam /smras pa ma yin no/smra pa de
6b6(1): bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par shes pa la gnas/smras pa/'jam dpal de ba zhin gshegs pa ni gang la yang myi gnas so/'jam dpal gyis smras ba/lha'i bu de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam
6b7(1): shes pa gang lags pa de la kho bo gnas pa shes par bya'o//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal khyod ci/de bzhin gshegs pa yin nam/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu de bzhin nyid kyi 'ong ba myed pa dang/'gro ba myed
6b8(1): pa gang yin ba de nyid/kho bo'i 'gro ba de nyid/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'gro ba ste/de na kho bo de bzhin gshegs pa'o/de bzhin nyid ci lta bar kho bo yang de ltar 'ong bas/des na kho bo de bzhin gshegasapa'o//de bzhin
6b9(1): nyid ci lta bar kho bo yangade ltar gnas te/des na kho bo de bzhin bshegs pa'o/lha'i bu 'jam dpal zhes bya ba 'di ni/de bzhin nyid ci lta ba de dra ste/des na kho bo de bzhin gi gshegs pa'o//de bzhin nyid sems myed cing pa'i
6b10(1): phyir/'jam dpal yang sems myed cing zhi ba ste/des na kho bo de ba zhin shegs pa'o//lha'i bus smras pa//'jam dpal de bzhin nyid gang tu btsal bar bya/'jam dpal gyis smras ba/lha'i bu de bzhin nyid ni
7a1(1): @#///lta bar song ba drug bcu rtsa gnyis rnams (las )btsal bar bya'o//lha'i bus smras ba lta bar song ba drug bcu pa gnyis po rnams gang tu btsal bar bya /'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i bu lta bar song ba drug bcurtsa gnyis 
7a2(1): po de dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa myi 'khrugs pa'i chos can gyi rnam par grol ba las 'tshol bar bya'o//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa'i/rnam par grol ba gang du btsal bar bya //'jam dpal gis
7a3(1): smras pa/lha'i bu de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'grol ba ni seMs can thams cad kyi spyod pa dang /bsam pa rnams su btsal bar bya'o //lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal sems can thams cad sems kyi spyod pa dang/
7a4(1): bsam pa gang tu btsal bar bya /'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i bu sangs rgyas kyi yul du'o//lha'i bus smras pa//'jam dpal sangs rgyas kyi yul gang kun 'tshal bar bya /'jam dpal(              ) /lha'i
7a5(1): bu sems can gi dbang po mchog dang/mchog ma yin pa thams cad shes pa las btsal bar bya'o//lha'i bus smras pa/ (                )/'jam dpal /khyod ci ltar 'dir gshegs pa nyung bar 'gyur ba de lta bur stond btam /
7a6(1): 'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu sangs rgyas kyiye shes sus kyang kun shes pa 'am/rnam par shes par myi nus so //de ci'i phyir zhe na/lha'i bu sangs rgyas kyi(         )ye shes ni chags pa myed bar rnam par 
7a7(1): rtog par myed pa/brjod du myed pa/snyand tu myed pa /sbyor ba myed pa /tshig myed pa /sems dang/yid dang /rnam par shes pa dang bral ba ste/des kyang kun shes bar bya pa dang /rnam par shes pa(           ) pa ni myi nus pa'i phyir 
7a8(1): ro //lha'i bus smras pa//'jam dpal gal te sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kun shes bar bya ba ma yin ni/nyan thos de dag gis kun shes bar ga la 'gyur /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyang phyir myi ldog pa la gnas bar ga la 
7a9(1): 'gyur /'jam dpal gyis smras pa lha'i bu gang yi ge (        )rnams kyi ye shes de bstand pa/'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi thabs te/yi shes 'di ni yi ge myed pa'o//lha'i bu 'di lta ste dper na mye btsubs pa 
7a10(1): las mye myi 'byung zhing /btsub cing btsubs pa las mye 'byung ba bzhin du/lha'i bu de bzhin gshegs bas gal te thog ma kho nar sangs rgyas kyi yi shes rgya che yang dag bar rab tu bstand na/sems can gang gis kyang de bzhin 
7b1(1): gshegs pa'i ye shes de kun shes pa myed par myi 'gyur bas /de bzhin gshegs bas btsub shing gi gtam ye shes de ni yi ge myed pa'o lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal gtsug shing gi gtam gang /'jam dpal gyis 
7b2(1): smras pa/lha'i bu gang ci yang rung ste/sdom ba dang yo byad snyungs pa'i gtam dang/pha rold tu phyind pa la sbyor pa'i gtam dang /byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos la 'jug pa'i gtam 'di ni btsub shing gi gtam zhes bya'o/lha'i
7b3(1): bus smras pa/'jam dbal gtsub shing gi gtam ma yin ba'i gtam gang /'jam dbal gis smras pa/lha'i bu gtam gang skye ba dang /'jig pa dang/gnas pa'i gnas gang yin ba dang /'drend pa dang seld ba'i gtaM
7b4(1): gang yin pa dang /gtam gang kun nas nyon mongs pa yongs su brjod pa'i gtam gang yin pa dang /rnam par byang ba'i phan yon gyi gtam ma yin pa dang /gtam gad 'khor ba la yid 'phyung ba'i gtam yin ba dang /mya ngan las 
7b5(1): 'das pa la mngon bar dga' bar bya ba gang yin pa'i gtam dang /gtam gar yongs su shes par bya ba'i phyir ma yin/spang bar bya pa'i phyir ma yin/sgom par bya pa'i phyir ma yin/mngon gsum du bya pa'i phyir
7b6(1): ma yin pa dang /gtam dang thob par bya pa'i phyir ma yin /mngon bar rtogs par bya pa'i phyir ma yin pa 'di ni/lha'i bu dag snying po yod pa'i gtam mo/lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal gtam 'di lta bu brjod
7b7(1): pa'i tshe/myig gis myi rtsol bar bya pa'i phyir/bdud sdig can 'dir myi 'ong ba ngo mtshard to//tshig de smras nas /ring por ma lon bar de'i mod la/bdud sdig can steng gi bar snang la srin chen po 'khrugs bar mngon bar su lad nas/
7b8(1): dgra chen po'i daro 'phyung ba dang/des thams cad dang ldan ba'i 'khor de skangs skrag nas /sgra skad kyi nga ro 'di lta bu 'di su zhig gyi snyam mo//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis 'jam dpal zho nur gyurd pa la bka' stsald pa/'jam dpal 
7b9(1): khyod kyis bdud sdig can gyi 'gyur pa mthong ngam/'jam dpal gis gsold pa/bcom ldan 'das mthong lags so//de nas 'jam dpal zho nur gyu rad bas /de'i tshe bdud sdig can bcing ba dam pos bcings te//sa'i sdang du lhung
7b10(1): ba de lta bu'i bsam ba bsams pa dang/bdag ni bcing ba dam pos bcing so/bdag gi bcing Idam pos bcings so zhes sgra 'byind to//'jam dpal gyis smraspa/sdig can bcing ba 'di bas ches mang pa'i bcing ba gzhan
8a1(1): //yod de/des khyod rtag tu bcings kyang khyod kyis khong du ma chud do//de yang gang zhe na /'di lta ste/nga 'o snyam pa'i nga rgyal gyi baching ba dang/phyin ci log gi bcing ba dang/sred pa dang /lta ba'i bcing 
8a2(1): ba ste/sdig can 'di ni bcing pa 'di bas ches dam pa'i bcing ba mang po gzhan yin te/des khyod rtag tu bcing gyang khongs su ma chud do//bdud kyis smras pa/'jam dpal thongs shing dang/bdag gi gnas su 'gro'o//
8a3(1): yang bdud kyi las myi bya'o/'jam dpal gyis smras pa /sdig can sangs rgyas kyis mdzad pa khyod kyis bya dgos na/khyod ci'i phyir gtang bdud kyis smras pa/'jam pa dpal bdud dag ni sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams la bar chad bya
8a4(1): ba'i phyir zhu sga pa yin na/bdag gis sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa 'jam dpal gyis smras pa/sdig can sangs rgyas gyis mdzad pa /'du 'byed du 'jug pa 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi
8a5(1): thams cad dang/shes rab kyi shes pa rnam bar spruld pa'o//yang dag bar sangs rgyas kyis sangs rgyas kyi mdzad ba mdzad pa'i ni don/tshar ma yin te//gang bdud sangs rgyas kyis mdzad pa'i byed pa 'di ni rab tu
8a6(1): ngo mtshar to//de nas 'jam dpal zho nur gyurd bas de'i tshe gang sdig can/sangs rgyas kyi gzungs dang ldan ba dang/sangs rgyas kyi seng ge'i khri la 'dug pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi spobs pa dang/ldan bar gyurd pa'i byin
8a7(1): kyi rlabs so//de nas 'dud sdig can gis/tshe dang ldan ba bdag ci dang ci 'dod pa kho po la dris shig dang/kho pos dris pa lung bstand bas/khyed kyi sems rangs bar bya'o//zhes tshig de skad smras so//de nas bdud
8a8(1): sdig can gyis/tshe dang ldan ba dag ci dang ci 'dod pa khro bo la dris shig dang/sdig can la 'di skad ce smras so//sdig can dge' slong rnal 'byor spyod pa'i 'ching gang yin/bdud kyis smras pa/btsun ba 'od srung chen po/
8a9(1): bsam gtan gyi ro myang bar byed ba ni/dge' slong rnal 'byor spyod ba'i 'ching ba'o//zhi gnas de la chags pa dang/'du shes myed 'du shes mying myis skye mched la snyoms bar 'jug pa dang/stong pa nyid la snying pos 'du shes pa dang/
8a10(1): lta bar song pa rnams la/bsal bar 'du shes pa dang/mtshan ma myed pa la snying por 'du shes pa dang /mtshan ma rnams la ka ru lad bar 'du shes pa dang/smond pa myed pa la/snying por 'du shes pa dang/smon lam la stsald
8b1(1): bar 'du shes pa dang/mya ngan las 'das pa la bstsald bar 'du shes ba dang/mya ngan las 'dasapa la snying por 'du shes pa dang/'khor ba la stsald bar 'du shes pa de dag ni//btsun ba 'od srung chen po dge' slong rnal dbyor
8b2(1): spyod pa'i mching ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na /btsun ba 'od srung chen pi lta bar song ba stsald nas stong pa nyid sgom bar bya ba ma yin gyi /lta bar song pa nyid stong pa nyid do//mtshan ma gsal nas /mtshan ma myed pa la sgom bar 
8b3(1): bya ba ma yin gyi//mtshan ma'i ngo bo nyid mtshan ma myed pa'o//smon pa myed pa la gsal nas /smond pa myed pa sgom bar bya ba ma yin gi//smon lam gyi ngo bo nyid kyi mtshan nyid kyis smond pa myed pa'o//'khor ba bsal
8b4(1): nas/mya ngan las 'das pa sgom bar bya ba ma yinate/'khor bar myigs pa nyid mya ngan las 'das pa'o//btsun ba 'od srung che na po /mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya ba ni/'du shes dang/rlom sems dang/mtshan ma la dmyi
8b5(1): gas ba thams cad la myi spyod pa ste//mya ngan las 'das pa ni skye ba dang/'jig pa dang /zag pa las ma yin gyi /gzhan gyis skye ba myed pa nyid /mya ngan las 'das ba'o//bstand pa 'di 'chad 
8b6(1): pa na /dge slong lnga brgya lend pa myed bar zag pa las se mas rnam bar grol lo//de nas gnas rtan rab byord kyis smras pa/tuld pa de ci lta bu /smras pa /ci ltar gnas pa nyid kyi phyir /cung 
8b7(1): zad kyang khrid pa yang myed do//ci ltar gnas gzhan du shes pa dang /skyed pa yang myed bka' ba yang myed /mnyam ba nyid du shes pa/de ltar bdag cag gtuld to /dge slong de dag gis /
8b8(1): bstand pa 'di bshad pa na /lha'i bu stong gnyi rgya chos rnams la chos kyi myig rdul myed cing dri ma dang bral ba rnam bar dag go//gnas rtan rab 'byord kyis /gzhan yang bdud sdig can la 'di skad ces smras so//
8b9(1): sdig can ci ltar na /dge slong sbyin gnas yin /bdud kyi smras pa/btsun ba rab 'byord nam dge slong lend pa yang myed /gnod pa yang myed la /dad cing dang ba thams cad las kyang/yongs su spyod pa na'o//btsun
8b10(1): ba rab 'byord gang dge slong gang la byin ba de la sgyu ma'i blo dang/gang lend pa de la dmyig g.yor gyi blo dang/nye bar lend pa'i sems dang/chos kyis kyang yongs su myi spyod pa'i dge' slong de lta bu ni sbyin gnas yin no//
9a1(1): @#///sha ra dwa ti'i bus smras pa/sdig can/bcom ldan 'das kyi ting nge 'dzin 'khrugs pa myed bar/gsungs pa gang yin/bdud kyis smras pa/byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzind gang la rtend
9a2(1): nas shind tu zad pa'i phyir zad pa yang zad bar myi byed/rang bzhin gyis ma skyes pa'i phyir/ma skyes ba yang myi skyed/myong ba myed pa yang myong bar myi byed de/chos thams cad kyang rang bzhin gyis snyoms bar zhugs bar shes 
9a3(1): la/shes nas kyang tshor thams cad/'gog pa'i snyoms bar 'jug pa la snyoms bar 'jug cing/mnyam bar yang mthong la/cung zad kyang mthong pa yang myed/myi mthong pa yang myed pa 'di bcom ldan 'das kyis ting nge 'dzind 
9a4(1): 'khrugs pa myed bar gzungs pa'o//me 'u 'gal gyi bus smras pa/sdig can ci ltar na/dge slong sems can thams cad la dbang thob pa/bdud kyis smras pa/btsun ba me'u 'gal gyi bu chen po/nam dge slong thams cad ya sems
9a5(1): rnam bar grol ba'i mtshan nyid du shes na rnam bar grol pa'i mtshan nyid la/mngon bar zhend pa yang myed cing/sems ma bzod nas rnam bar grol pa'i sems la/baching bar yang myi byed/grol bar yang myi byed/sems gzugs myed 
9a6(1): bas/chos thams cad kyang gzugs myed bar shes/sems gan/sa pa myed bas/chos thams cad gnas pa myed bar mthong/sems 'a/dzind pa myed bas/chos thamasacad 'dzind pa re myed bar shes kyang sems kyis
9a7(1): sems shes pa yang myed la/sems kyi ngo bo nyid kyi rang bzhin gyis chos thams cad kyi chos kyi dbyings kyi ngo bo nyid kyi rang bzhin gyis gzhan gyi dring la myi 'ardog bar dbang po thob pa ni rdzu 'phrul dang ldan pa yin te/btsun ba me'u 'gal 
9a8(1): gyi bu chen po/dge slong de lta bu ni chos thams cad la dpang thob ba yin no//gang pos smras pa/sdig can ci ltar na/dge slong yongs su dag bar chos stand pa(sa) ni/bdud kyis smras pa /btsun ba gang po nam/dge slong gzhan la(sa)
9a9(1): chos thams cad shes kyang /gzhan ma yin pa'i sems la mngon bar zhend pa yang myed/chos thams cad kyang bsgro btags pa myed bar stond/yi ge dang/skad dang/dbyangs dang tshig lam thams cad kyang brag cha lta bur shes/chos nyand pa tha
9a10(1): mas cad kyang sgyu ma lta bur thong/rang gi lus kyi rnam par rigs byed kyang chu zla lta bur mthong/nyon mongs pa thams cad yongs su rtogs bar las 'byung bar shes/chos 'ga' zhig gzung ba'i phyir ram/'dor pa'i phyir ram/shind tu zla bas phyir
9b1(1): ram /thob bar bya ba'i phyir yang chos myi stond /so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi bo rnams la yang dbang thob cing legs so /zhes bya ba la yang re ba myed pa la zang zing myed bar chos stond cing /rang gi sems rang bzhin gyis yongs su dag bas /
9b2(1): sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi rang bzhin gyis yongs su bdag bar shes te /sems kyi rang bzhin gyis yodas su dag bar shes nas /phung po'i bdud la dben bar mthong pa dang /nyon mongs pa'i bdud la bcos bar 'du shes pa dang /'chi bdag gi 
9b3(1): bdud la skyes pa dang /myi skye bar gnas pa dang /lha'i bu bdud la g.yo' ba dang /rlom sems thams cad dang bral bar 'gyur ba yin te /dge slong de lta bu ni yongs su dag bar chos stond pa yin no//nye bar 'khor gyis smras pa /sdig can ji ltar na /
9b4(1): dge slong 'dul pa 'dzind pa yin /bdud kyis smras pa /btsun pa nye bar 'khor dge slong gi nam chos thams cad shind tu dul bar shes te /nyes pa rnams kyi thog ma'i gyi tha' sngon yang dben pa'i tha mar shes la /nyes byung ba rnams kyi bgyod pa yang 
9b5(1): seld cing /dam phor myi byed de /de dag la gang gis 'tshams myed pa /rnams kyang rnam bar gnod la /cho ga dang /tshul khrims la log bar zhugs pa phran tshegs lta dgos kyang ci de lta bu'i chos kyi tshul gyi rnam 
9b6(1): pa gang nye bar bsgrub /nyon mongs pa 'dul ba la yang 'khas /'gro bur gyi nyon mongs pa rnams la yang kun nas nyon mongs bar 'gyur ba gang yin ba dang /'byung bar 'du shes nyon mongs pa nang du yang myi gtod /phyi 
9b7(1): rold du yang ma yin /gnyi ga myed bar ma yang ma yin te /nyon mongs pa khor du chud pa ni yin /nyon mongs pa myed bar shes shing /nyon mongs pa yang myi seld /nyon mongs pa myed par yang myi grub /nyon mongs pa thams cad kyang srin dang /
9b8(1): char srin 'dra bar shes te /ye shes dang lhag mthong gyi rlung gyis gtord nas /phyogs dang yul du 'gro ba yang myed /gnas pa yang myed pa dang /nyon mongs pa rnams ni chu zla dang 'dra ste /yongs su rtogs ba'i gzugs brnyan gyis /bzhin 
9b9(1): nye bar stond pa dang /nyon mongs pha rnams ni mun pa'i spyod yul te /shes rab kyi sgron mas myi snang ba snang bar byed pa dang /nyon mongs med pa rnams ni chom rkun dang /mkha' 'gro ma dang /gnod sbyin dang /srin mo dang 'dra ste /
9b10(1): tshul bzhin yid la byed bas rtags na gnas pa ma yin ba dang /nyon mongs pa rnam ni glags tshol ba ste /tshul bzhin ma yin pa yid la byed bas 'phel ba dang /stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma myed pa dang /smond pa myed pa'i shes bas 
10a1(1): //yid la 'jug par myi byed par sus nyon mongs pa rnams 'di 'dra bar shes te/sems nyon mongs pas/kun nas nyon mongs pa rnams la/snying rje zhing bdag myed par/sems can myed pa mngon gsum du gyurd
10a2(1): pa na/'dul ba 'dzind pa na/yang dag pa zhes bya'o/de ltar nyan thos chen po de dag thams cad kyis rang rang gyi 'dod pa/bdud sdig can de la kun 'dris nas/bdud sdig can gyis kyang de dag thams cad kyi don bstand la 'bab pa stan
10a3(1): nas/sems dangs bar gyurd to//de nas lha'i bu mtshan bzangs 'khor de nyid du 'dus par 'gyur te 'dug pa des bdud sdig can de la 'di skad ces smras so/sdig can 'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd bas ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams
10a4(1): kyi bdud kyi las bshad zin nas/khyod kyang spobs par gyis/sdig can byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi bdud kyi las gang /de skad ces smras pa dang/bdud sdig can gyis/lha'i bu mtshan bzangs la 'di skad ces smras so //
10a5(1): lha'i bu nyi shu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa'i rnams kyi bdud kyi las yang dag 'phul yin no//nyi shu gang zhe na 'di lta ste/rnal 'byor spyod pa rnam par grong pa bar 'dod cig/'khor bas dkrag pa rnams snyend pa dang
10a6(1): stend pa dang/snyen bkur byed pa ni bdud kyi las so//stong pa nyid la so sor rtog cig sems can yal bar dor bar yang bdud kyi las so //'dus ma byas la so sor rtog cing/'dus byas kyi dge ba'i
10a7(1): rtsa bas yongs su skyo ba bdud kyi las so//bsam gtan skyed cing /bsam gtan la rnam par rtog pa tshol ba yang bdud kyi las so//chos bstond kyang chos nyand pa rnams la snying rje chen po myi skyed pa yang bdud
10a8(1): kyi las so//yon dang ldan pa rnams la sbyin nas tshol zhing/tshul khrims 'chald pa rnams la khro ba yang bdud kyi las so /nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas kyi bstam stond cing/theg pa chen po'i gtam chad pa yang bdud kyi
10a9(1): las so//zab mo'i gtam 'chad 'acing sna tshogs bstond pa yang bdud kyi las so/byang chub sems dpa'i lam shes kyang pha rold tu phyind pa'i lam yongs su myi 'tshol ba yang bdud kyi las swo//rab tu 'dod pa'i bsngags
10a10(1): pa brjod kyang sems can myi brtson pa rnams/mngon par myi sbyor ba rnams yang bdud kyi las so/brtsa ba stsogs kyang byang chub sems dpa' mngon tu myi byed pa yang bdud kyi las swo //lhag mthong dang/rnal 'byor dang/
10b1(1): ldan bar gnas kyang sems can rnams lhag mthong yang dag par 'dzind tu 'jug pa yang bdud kyi las so//nyon mongs pa ma lus bar zad pa tshol zhing khor ba'i rgyun gyi nyon mongs pa rnams la smod pa yang bdud kyi las so //shes rab 
10b2(1): kyis rnam bar spyod cing snying rje chen po la dmyigs bar myi 'dzind pa yang bdud kyi las so//thabs dang myi ldan bar dag dge ba spyod pa thams cad kyang bdud kyi las so//byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod myi 'tshol gyi /'jig 
10b3(1): rten rkyang phan ba'i gsang chig 'dzind pa yang bdud kyi las so//mang du thos kyang gzhan gyis shes kyis togs te chos la bslob sron dpe' khyud byed pa yang bdud kyi las sa so//'jig rtend pa'i gos pa'i zhal ta byed cing 
10b4(1): mang du thos pa myi 'tshol pa yang bdud kyi las so//byang chub sems dpa' chos smra pa theg pa chen po la yang dag bar zhugs pa /bskald pa mnyam pa rnams la myi bsten myi snyend /bsnyen bkur myi byed kyi //nyan 
10b5(1): thos dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa la yang bar zhugs pa /skald pa myi mnyam ba la dga' pa yang bdud kyi las so /gang gi tshe brgya byin nam /tshangs pa 'am /'jig rten skyong ba 'am /
10b6(1): rgyal po 'am/'tshon dpon nam/khyim bdag gi long spyod dang /nor dang /rin po che dang /dbang phyug gi tshe ba nyid du gyurd pa de'i tshe /chos kyi che ba nyid dang ldan ba rnams la myi rtend /myi snyend 
10b7(1): bsnyen bkur myi byed pa 'di dag ni lha'i bu byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bdud kyi las yang dag phul nyi shu'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis bdud sdig can la/sdig can khyod kyis byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyis bdud 
10b8(1): kyi las 'di dag legs bar bshad pa ni legs so//legs so /zhes bya ba byin no/byang chub sems dpa' gang dag bdud kyi las 'di rnams thos shing /thos nas kyang rnam bar spong ba de dag /byang chub sems dpa'
10b9(1): yongs su smyind bar byed pa'i chos nyi shu thob bar byed do/nyi shu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/byams pa chen po dang /snying rje chen po dang /'khor na yongs su myi skyo ba dang /dge pa'i bshes gnyen gyis yongs su 
10b10(1): 'dzind pa dang /sangs rgyas 'byung ba la mnyes par byed pa dang /pha rold tu phyind pa rnams nyand pa dang /byang chub sems dba'i 'khor dang /bskald pa mnyam pa dang /zungs rab tu thob ba dang /sgo bas 
11a1(1): @##///pa bun gsum tshogs pa dang/mngon bar shes pa'i shes pa dang/chos ma thos pa nyand pa dang /tshe rabs tshe rabs (4)su byang chub kyi sems mngon tu gyurd pa dang/rtag tu rgyun myi chad bar rab tu 'byung ba rnyed pa dang/dal 
11a2(1): ba phun gsum tshogs pa dang/ci ltar thos pa'i chos rnams bsgrubs pa dang/thabs dang shes rab kyi shes bas sems can 'dud cing yongs su 'dzind pa dang/dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzind pa dang /g.yo' myed cing sgyu myed pa dang/phangs 
11a3(1): pa'i dngos po thams cad yongs su gtong pa dang/sems can thams cad la khong khro ba myed pa ste/byang chub sems dpa' yongs su smyind bar byed pa'i chos nyi shu po 'di dag thob bar 'gyur ro//de nas lha'i (4)mtshan bzangs kyis /bdud 
11a4(1): sdig can la 'di skad ces smras so/sdig can khyod la bcom ldan 'das kyis legs so zhes bya ba gnang ba ni /sdig can khyod kyis snyed pa legs pa rnyed do/bdud kyis smras pa /lha'i bu 'di la kho bos rnyed pa ci zhig legs bar 
11a5(1): rnyed/de ci'i phyir zhe na/lha'i bu 'di lta ste /dper na/myi gang 'byung po 'bab bas bstand pa de ni/myi de ni de'i 'gyur ba ma yin gi/de ni 'byung po de'i 'gyur bar lta'o/lha'i bu de bzhin du kho bo yang 'jam dpal
11a6(1): gyis /byin kyis rlabs nas stond (kyi) 'di kho bo'i skyes ba btsal du myi lta'o//lha'i bus smras pa/sdig can khyod sangs rgyas kyi gzugs kyi mtshan dang ldan zhing sangs rgyas kyi I seng ge'i khri la 'dug te/
11a7(1): sangs rgyas kyi spos(4) ba dang /ldan zhing chos bstond bas dga' bar ma gyurd tam /bdud kyis smras pa khyod kyis ni kho bo rgyand bar mthong ba lta na /kho bos ni bcings pa dam bos bdag bcings bar shes so//lha'i bus
11a8(1): smras pa sdig can 'jam dpal gzhonur gyurd pa la bzod pa gsol cig /de nas gtong bar 'gyur ro//de nas 'jam dpal gyi byin kyi rlabs kyis lha'i bu mtshan bzangs la 'di skad ces smras so//lha'i bu byang chub sems dpa' sems
11a9(1): dpa' chen po la yang dag bar bzhugs pa bzod gsol myi dgos so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de dag la 'khrug pa myed/khro ba myed pa'i phyir ro /gang bdag 'khrug cing khro ba de dag la ni bzod pa gsol (5)gos so//lha'i bus smras pa/
11a10(1): sdig can byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bzod pa ci dra//bdud kyis smras pa /lha'i bu byang chub sems dba' rnams kyi bzod pa ni/rnam pa bcu gnyis te/rnam bcu gnyis gang zhe na/'di lta ste /bcos ma ma yin
11b1(1): bsam bsam pa'i bzod pa dang/khong khro ba myed pa'i sems kyis lhag pa'i bsam pa'i bzod pa dang/sems can thams cad la myi slu bas g.yo' pa myed pa'i bzod pa dang/dbul zhing 'phongs pa'i yongs su skyob bas snying rtse bzhis snying rtse ba'i
11b2(1): bzod pa dang/ldog pa myed pa'i ci bskad smras ba de bzhin du byed pa'i bzod pa dang/lta bar song ba dang/pral bas stong pa nyid kyi bzod pa dang /chos nyid la stand bas chos nyes bar sems pa'i bzod pa dang /bdag myed pa'i bzad mo'i bzod
11b3(1): pa dang/'phags pa'i skye bo'i rjes su 'jug bas mthun pa'i bzod pa dang/rten cing 'breld bar 'byung ba dang/mthun bas yang dag pa'i bzod pa dang/sems can thams cad yongs su srung bas/ngo bo byed pa yi yang myed la myi mthun ba yang myed pa'i
11b4(1): bzod pa dang/myi skye ba chos las bzod pa rab tu thob pa'i phyir/skye ba myed cing 'byung pa myed ba'i bzod pa ste/lha'i bu 'di lta bu 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnam pa bcu gnyis so/lha'i bus smras pa/sdig can khyod gal te bkrol nas /
11b5(1): dga' bar 'gyur ram/bdud kyis smras pa/lha'i bu kho bo dga' ste/mchog tu dga' 'o/de nas lha'i bu mtshan bzangs kyis 'jam dpala zho nur gyurd pa la/'di skad ces smras so/'jam dpal bdud
11b6(1): sdig can mthong shig dang/rang gi gnas su gro'o/de nas 'jam dpalgzho nur gyurd bas/bdud sdig can la 'di skad ces smras so/sdig cankhyod su zhig gis bcings gang nas grol//bdud kyis smras pa/
11b7(1): 'jam dpal dag ni mus bcings myi shes so//'jam dpal gyis smras pa/sdig can ci ltar khyod bcings pa myed la/bcings bar 'du shes pa de bzhin du byis pa so so'i skye bo thams cad kyang myi rtag pa la rtag bar 'du shes pa/sdug bsngal pa la
11b8(1): bde bar 'du shes pa/bdag myed pa la bdag tu 'du shes/myi sdug pa la sdug par 'du shes/gzugs myed pa la gzugs su 'du shes/tshong ba myed pa dang/'du shes myed pa dang/'du byed rnams dang/rnam bar shes pa myed pa dang/
11b9(1): tshor ba dang/'du shes dang/'du byed rnams da/rnam bar shes bar 'du shes so//sdig can khyod grol na gang nas grol/bdud kyis smras pa/bdag ni gang nas kyang myi grol lo/smras pa sdig can/de bzhin du gang dag rnam bar grol
11b10(1): bar yang dag pa ma yin pa'i 'du shes yongs su shes pa na/ma gags bar gang la yang/rnam bar 'gyur pa myed de/de dag yongs su shes bas/rnam bar grol ba zhes bya'o//de nas 'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd bas/byind kyis rlabs
12a1(1): @#///de bsdus pa dang /sdig can de rang gi gzugs dang /ldan bar rang gyis shes so /de nas gnas bstan 'od srung chen bos /bdud sdig can la 'di skad smras so /sdig can khyod kyis sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa byas pa legaso legs 
12a2(1): so /bdud kyis smras pa btsun ba 'od srung chen bos /'di ni kho bo'i yul ma yin gi 'di ni 'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pa'i yul du lta'o //de nas lha'i bu mtshan bzangs kyis /'jam dpal gzho nur gyud pa la 'di skad ces smras so /'jam
12a3(1): dpal sangs rgyas kyi 'dzad pa gang tu 'tshal bar bya /'jam dpal gyis smras pa /lha'i bu sangs rgyas kyi 'dzad pa ni sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa thaMs cad du gtsal bar bya'o //de ci'i phyir zhe na /gal te sems can rnams kyi nyon mongs 
12a4(1): bas gzird bar byed na /sangs rgyas kyis kyang mdzad bar mi 'gyur ba'i phyir ro //lha'i bu 'di lta ste dper na /gal te sems can dam rnams na bar ma gyurd na /sman bas kyang bya bar myi 'gyur ba de bzhin du /lha'i bu gal te sems 
12a5(1): can rnams nyon mongs pas gzird bar ma gyurd na /sangs rgyas kyis kyang mdzad bar myi 'gyur ro /lha'i bus smras pa /'jam dpal gang byung na sangs rgyas byung /'jam dpal gyis smras ba /lha'i bu
12a6(1): skye ba dang /rga ba dang /na ba dang /'chi ba byung na sangs rgyas sa 'byung ngo //de ci'i phyir zhe na/chos de dag spang pa'i don /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams 'byung ba'i phyir ro /lha'i bus smras
12a7(1): pa /'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs bas byang chub rnyes nas /chos skyed pa de gang /chos bkag pa gang /'jam dpal gyis smras pa /lha'i bu de bzhin gshegs bas byangachub sems dpa' rnyes nas /chos 
12a8(1): gang yang skye ba yang myed /bkag ba yang myed do /de ci'i phyir zhe na /lha'i bu chos thams cad skye ba ma yin /'gag pa myed pa'i phyir ro /lha'i bu sangs rgyas 'byung ba /sangs rgyas 'byung ba zhes bya ba 'di ni /
12a9(1): rang bzhin gyis ngo bo nyid kyis skye ba myed pa'i tshig bla dgas so //lha'i bus smras pa /'jam dpa' ci ltar na lhag pa'i bsam pas phun gsum tshogs pa yin /'jam dpal gyis smras pa /gang dmyigs pa'i lta bas chos 
12a10(1): gang la yang kun tu myi spyod pa'o /lha'i bus smras pa /ji ltar na lhag pa'i bsam bas phun gsum tshogs pa yin /'jam dpal byis smras pa /gang nang dang /phyi'i chos rnams la chags pa myed do /lha'i bus smras pa/
12b1(1): ci ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sbyin pa phun gsum tshogs pa yin/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/gang nyon mongs pa thams cad kyang dor la/sems can thams cad kyang yongs su myi gtong /lha'i bus smras pa/ci ltar na byang chub 
12b2(1): sems dpa' tshul khrims phun gsum tshogs pa yin /'jam dpal gyis smras pa /ci ltar na stond pas bstand pa bzhin du bsgrub cing /sems can thams cad kyi tshul khrims la 'god/lhag pa'i bsam bas byang chub sems kyang myi 'dor pa'o/
12b3(1): lha'i bus smras pa/ci ltaran byang chub sems dpa' bzod pa phun gsum tshogs pa yi 'jam dpal gyis smras pa/gang chos thams cad shind tu za ba pa'i mtshan nyid du shes nas /sems can thams cadakyi gnod sems dang /khro ba dang 'thab pa 
12b4(1): spang ba'i phyir /thams cad mkhyend pa nyid kyi go cha myi dor ba'o/lha'i bus smras pa /ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rtson grus phun gsum tshogs pa yis/'jam dpal gyis smras ba/nam byang chub sems dpa' chos gang gyi phyir brtson
12b5(1): 'grus rtsod pa'i chos de ni lta ste/bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub ste/de nyid shind tu yang dag par rjes su myi mthong na/sems can thams cad ki le lo spang ba'i phyir /brtson 'grus ces bya yang myi 'dor na'o/
12b6(1): lha'i bus smras ba/ci ltar na byang chub sems pa' bsam gtan du na gsum tshogs pa yin /'jam dpal gyis smras pa /nam chos thams cad rang bzhin gyis snyoms par 'jug par shes kyang /sems can thams cad
12b7(1): kyi nyon mongs pa spong ba'i 'dul ba'i phyir yang bsam gtan byed pa na'o//lha'i bus smras pa/ci ltar na byang chub semad dpa' shes rab phun gsum tshogs pa yin/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/nam rgyu myed pa thob bar gyurd te/spros pa
12b8(1): thams cad dang bral zhing /sems can thams can kyi lta ba spang pa'i phyir /shes rab kyis rnam par spyod pa na ste//de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' shes rab phun gsuM tshogs pa yin no/lha'i bus smras pa /ci ltar na byang chub ses dpa'
12b9(1): byams pa la gnas pa yin/'jam dpal kyis smras pa/nam chos thams cad rangabazhin gyis mya ngan las 'das par mthong na'o/lha'i bus smras pa /ciltar na byang chub sems dba' snying rje la gnas pa yin /'jam dpal gyis smras ba/
12b10(1): nam chos thams cad la las kyi rnam par sbyind pa myed par shes na'o/lha'i bus smras ba /ciltar na byang chub seMs dpa' dga' ba la gnas pa yin/'jamadapal gyis smras pa /nam chos thams cad la dga' ba yang myi skyed la/myi dga'
13a1(1): @#///ba yang ma yin no/lha'i bus smras pa/ci ltar na byang chub sems dpa' btang snyoms la gnas pa yin /'jam dpal gyis smras pa/nam chos thams cad gnyis dang bral bar 'gyur ba na'o/lha'i bus smras pa/
13a2(1): ci ltar na byang chub sems pa' yang /byang chub sems dpa' yang dga' ba zhes bya /'jam dpal gyis smra pa /chos thams cad sgyu ma lta bur byung ba myed bar shes pa na'o/lha'i bus smras pa /ci ltar na byang chub 
13a3(1): sems dpa' chen po yin/'jam dpal gyis smrasapa/nam byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad bdag myed bar mthong ba na'o//lha'i bus smras pa/ci lta na byang chub sems dpa' sems can gi mchog yin/'jam dpa

13b3(1): byas pa shes pa yin/'jam dpal gyis sgras pa/nam theg pa gzhan la 'dod bar myi byed pa na'o//lha'i bus smras pa//ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'dod pa nyung ba yin/'jam dpal gyisasmras pa/nam rang gi nyon mongs pa rnams

13b5(1): chos thams cad myi land pa na'o//lha'i bus smra sa pa/ci ltar byang chub se mas dpa' ngoms pa yin/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/nam 'jig rten las 'das pa'i shes rab la gnas la 'jig rten pa'i rgyu bas sems can rnams

13b7(1): thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa spang pa'i phyir/brtson bar na yang snang ba na'o/lha'i bus smras pa/ci ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rang dbang can yin/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/nam zag pa myed pa'i lus kyis sangs rgyas kyi zhing

13b9(1): pa'i chos thos bar byed pa na'o//lha'i bu sa smras pa/ji ltar na byangs chub sems dpa' tshul bzhan la brtson ba yin/'jam dpal gis smras pa/nam dmyigs pa thams cad las 'byung ba rab tu shes kyang/chos thams cad 'phros bar myi byed/myi 'a
13b10(1): phro bar yang myi byed pa na'o//lha'i bus smrasapa/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bsgrub pa snying bor byed ba yin//'jam dpal gyis smras pa/nam gzhan dang 'breld pa'i rnam bar rig pa myi skyed cing/sems can thams cad
14a1(1): @# //kyis sems kyi spyod pa dben bar yang rtog la /rang gyi sems kyi spyod pa la yang myi lta zhing /bdag dang gzhan du 'du shes bas bsgrub pa snying por byed pa ma yin ba na'o/lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal ji ltar na/
14a2(1): byang chub sems dpa' gzungs dang/spobs pa phun gsum tshogs pa yin /'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu nam byang chub sems dpa' thos pa thams cad kyang 'dzind la/sems can thams cad kyi dbang po rnams la yang tshungs
14a3(1): bar rab tu byed cing sgra skad brag cha dang /mtshungs bar yang shes nas/mngon bar zhend pa myed pa na ste/de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gzugs dang/spos pa phun gsum tshogs pa min no//de nas lha'i bu 'od mang pos/
14a4(1): 'jam dpal zho nur gyurd pa la 'di skad ces smras so//'jam dpal khyod kyis bshad pa'i don 'di sus kun shes bar 'gyur //'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i bu gang dag dge' pa'i gshes gnyen gyis yongs su
14a5(1): zind bar gyurd pa/bsngon gyi sbyor ba phun gsum tshogsa pa/dge pa'i rtsa ba skyed pa/dmyigs pa myed bar spyod pa de dag/gis/kho bos bshad pa'i don 'di kun shes bar 'gyur ro//lha'i bu
14a6(1): sa smras pa/de dag ci las mos bar 'gyur/bsam ba ni gang/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/de dag ni zab mo la mos bar gyurd te/lhag pa'i nga rgyal ba myed pa ni bsam pa'o//lha'i bus smras pa/
14a7(1): 'jam dpal ji ltar na dge slong lhag pa'i nga rgyal myed pa yin/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu nam dge slong 'jigs tshogs la lta ba'i ngo po nyid kyis bgrod pa gcig lta bu'i lam du sgom ste/gnyis su myed 
14a8(1): bas 'jigs tshogs la lta ba'i do bo nyid gang yin ba de bgrod pa cig bu'i lam gyi ngo bo nyid du mnyam bar shes shing /ma rig pa dang/srid pa dang/sred pa las byung ba yang 'tshol la/ma rig ba dang/srid pa dang/srid pa las
14a9(1): byung pa so sor rtogs ba na/ma rig pa dang/sridapa dang/sred pa'i ngo bo nyid kyang rig pa dang/rnam bar grol bar shes bar lta ste/de ltar na dge slong lhag pa' ི nga rgyal myed pa yin no/lha'i bu gzhan yang /dge slong
14a10(1): 'dod 'chags kyi mtha' la/'dod chags dang bral ba'i mthar shes shing /'dod chags dang bral pa'i mtha'i rang bzhin yang /'dod chags su shes pa dang/zhe sdang gi mtha' la /zhe sdang dang bral pa'i mthar shes shing /zhe sdang dang bral
14b1(1): pa'i rang bzhin yang zhe sdang du shes pa dang/gti mug gi mtha' la garti mug dang bral pa'i mthar shes shing /rti mug dang bral pa'i mthang rang bzhin yang rti mug tu shes pa na ste/de ltar na dge slong lhag pa'i nga rgyal myed pa yin no//lha'i bu gzhan 
14b2(1): yang nam dge slong sdug bsngal yongs su shes par myi byed//kun 'byung ba spong bar myi byed/'gog pa mngon gsum du myi byed/lam sgom bar yang myi byed la/sdug bsngal myi skye ba shes bas/'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi brjes su rtog
14b3(1): ste/sdug bsngal myi skye ba zhes pa gang yin ba de la//kun byung ba (gang )yang myed/sdug bsngal myi skye pa shes pa ni/gang yin ba de la/'gog pa gang yang myed/sdug bsngal myi skya ba shes pa/gang yin ba de la/lam gang yang myed pa de ltar 
14b4(1): dge slong tshul bzhin la rtson ba ni chos gang yang skye bar myi byed/'gag bar myi byed gnas bar myi byed do/gang la skye ba myed pa dang/'gag pa myed pa dang/gnas myed pa de 'I dus ma byas pa'o//'dus ma byas kyi rang bzhin yang 'phags pa 
14b5(1): rnams kyi bden ba sgom ba ste/lha'i bu de ltar na/dge slong lhag pa'i nga rgyal myed pa yin no//de nas bdud sdig can sdug bsngal zhing yid myi bde ste//rdod mchi' mas gang ste/mchi' mas brnangs pa bzhin du/tshig 'di skad 
14b6(1): ces smras so//gang na chos kyi rnams grangs 'di spyod pa de na bdud/rnams skams myed do//gang dag chos rnams kyi rnam grangs de/'dzind bar//gyurd pa de dag gis bdud kyi las thams cad bkag par 'gyur ro//zhes 
14b7(1): de bskad smras nas/bdud sdig can sdug bsngalazhing yid myi bde ste/yid la bcags pa bzhin de nyid du myi snang bar gyurd to//de nas lha'i bu 'od mang bos/'jam dpal zho nur gyurd pa la 'di skad ces smras so//
14b8(1): 'jam dpal khyod kyis bshad pa'i don ji ltar bdag gis shes pa ltar/de dag lhag pa'i nga rgyal myed pa'i tshul zab mo 'dis sa nas/spong pa'i dge slong de dag gi rab tu byung ba myed do//snyend bar rdzogs pa myed do//lhag pa'i nga rgyal
14b9(1): myed pa'i tshul mchog tu bzab pa 'di thos nas myi skrag/myi dngang la dngang bar myi 'gyur zhing/mos bar 'gyur ba de dag rnam bar grol ba nyid du rig bar bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis/lha'i bu 'od mang po la 'di skad ces bka'
14b10(1): bstsald to//lha'i bu de de bzhin no/ji skad smras pa bzhin te/dge slong dam/dge' slong ma 'am/dge bsnyen nam/dge snyen ma gang dag lhag (pa'i)rgyal myed pa'i tshul mchog tu zab pa 'di/thos na myi skrag myi dngang la/
15a1(1): //dngang bar myi 'gyur ba de dag/rnam bar grol ba nyid du rig par bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/bzod pa 'dis byang chub sems dpa'/rnams bla na myed pa yang brjod rdzogs pa'i byang chub du lung bstand pa dang/bzod pa 'dis nyan thos
15a2(1): dang/rang sangs rgyas thams cad la nges par 'byung pa'i phyir ro//lha'i bu 'od mang pos gsold pa/bcom ldan 'das chos kyi rnam grangs/'di 'i mying ci lags/'di ci ltar bzung bar bgyi/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsald pa/lha'i
15a3(1): bu de'i phyir khyod kyis chos kyi rnam grag 'di 'jam dpal rnam par stsal ba'i le'u zhes bya bar zungs shig/bdud 'dul ba'i le'u zhes bya bar zangs shig/bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsald nas/'jam dpal zho nur gyurd pa dang lha'i
15a4(1): bu 'od mang po dang/thams cad ldan pa'i 'khor de dang/lha dang/myi dang/lha ma yin dang/dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yid rangs te/bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par bstod do////'phags pa rnam par

15a10(1): 'bring kyi 'dus kyi tshe nang du yang dag 'jog pa las bzhengs nas/'khor mang pos yongs su bskord cing/mdun kyis bltas nas tshos bstond to//de nas 'dzam dpal zho nur gyur pas/'chag cing dge slong la rgya po thams cad kyi gnas
15a11(1): sa khang gnas khang du 'byung ste//gnas stan sha ri'i bu gnas khang ga la ba der song ste phyind nas/'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pas/gnas rtan sha ri'i bu shind tu dben par 'dug ste/nang dag bzhag nas bsam gtan
15b1(1): byed bar mthong ngo /mthong nas gnas btan sha ri'i bu la 'di skad ces smras so /btsun ba sha ri'i bus khyod bsam byed dam/smras pa 'jam dpal de bzhin no/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/btsun ba sha ri'i bu khyod nyon 
15b2(1): mongs pa spangs pa rnams /swa ra pa'i phyir bsam gtan byed dam/'on te ma spangs pa rnams spang ba'i phyir bsam gtan byed/ci btsun ba sha ri'i bu khyod 'das pa la gnas te bas gtan byed dam/'on ta ma 'ongs pa la gnas
15b3(1): te bsam gtan byed dam//'on te de ltar 'byung ba las gnas te bsam gtan byed/btsun ba sha ri'i bu khyed gzugs la gnas te/bsam gtan byed/'on te tshor ba dang/'du shes dang/rnam par shes pa la gnas te bsam 
15b4(1): gtan byed/ci btsun pa sha ri'i bu khyod mig la gnas te bsam gtan bye dam/'on te rna ba dang/sna dang/lce dang/lus dang/yid la gnas te bsam gtan byed/ci btsun pa sha ri'ibu khyod gzugs la gnas te/
15b5(1): bsam gtan byed dam/'ine te sgra dang/dri dang/ro dang /reg bya ba dang/chos rnams la gnas te bsam gtan yid dam/btsun pa sha ri'i bu khyoda'adod pa'i khamam la gnas te bsam gtan yed dam/'on te
15b6(1): gzugs kyi khams dang/gzugs myed pa'i khams la gnas te bsam gtad byed/ci btsun ba khyod/nang la gnas te bsam gtan pa byed dam/'on te phyi rold la gnas te gtam byed dam/'on te phyi nang la gnas
15b7(1): te bsam gtan byed/ci btsun ba sha ri'i bu khyod/gzugs la gnas te bsam gtane byed/'on te sems la gnas te bsam ga tan byed/sha ri'i bus smras pa 'jam dpal tshe 'di la bde bar gnas bar byed pa dang/brjed bar 
15b8(1): myed bar gnas bar bya ba'i phyir te/de lta bu'i phyir bsam gtan byed do/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/btsun ba sha ri'i bu chos gang dag tshe 'di la bde bar gnasapa dang/tshe 'di ma yin pa la bde bar gnas pa 'am/brjed pa myed pa'i 
15b9(1): chos de dag dmyigs sam/sha ri'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal bdag gis chos gang dag tshe 'di la  bde bar gnas pa dang/tshe 'di ma yinapa la bde bar gnas pa'i chos de dag yang dag bar rjes su myi mthong zhing/myi dmyigs mod kyi/
15b10(1): 'on kyang 'jam dpal/de bzhin gshegs pa nyan thos rnams la/rab tu dben ba'i chos su gang gsungs pa gang yin ba de/de la bdag brten cing gnas so/'jam dpal gyis smrasapa/'jam dpal 'di la dge slong 'das 
16a1(1): @#///pa la brten cing gnas so//ma 'ongs pa la brten cing gnas so//da ltar 'byung ba la rten cing gnas so//de bzhin du sbyar te/sems kyi bar la bar la brtend cing gnas bar khong du chud bar bya ste 'jam dpal/
16a2(1): /chos de dag ni de bzhin gshegs bas nyan thos rnams la/rab tu dben bar gsungs pa yin te/bdag chos de dag la brten cing gnas so/'jam dpal gyis smras/btsun ba sha ri'i bu gang gi phyir/'di skad ces 'das pa la
16a3(1): brten cing gnas/ma 'ongs la brten cing gnas/da ltar byung ba la rtend cing rab tu gnas pa la gnas nas/de bzhin du sbyar te/sems kyi bar la rten cing rab tu dben ba la gnas zhes smra/btsun ba sha ri'i bu 'di lta ste/
16a4(1): gang 'das ba'i de bzhin nyid de yang yod pa ma yin/ma 'ongs pa'i de bzhin nyid de yang yid pa ma yin/gang da ltar 'byung pa'i de bzhin nyid de yangayod ma yin te/de ltar chos de dag ni yod pa ma yin/ci ltar gnas gtan sha
16a5(1): ri'i bu da ci skad ces 'das ba la brten cing gnas/ma 'ongasapa la yang brten cing gnas/da ltar 'byung ba la yang brten cing gnas shes smra/yod pa ma yin pa'i chos la ni gnas myed do/yang btsun pa
16a6(1): sha ri'i bu 'das pa'i de bzhin nyid dang/ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin nyid gang yin ba de ni ci yang ma yin/gang gis kyang ma yin/gang gi yang ma yin/gang la yang myi gnas te/gang myi gnas
16a7(1): dmyigs su myed do/yang btsun ba sha ri bu gang 'das pa dang/ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar 'byung pa'i de bzhind cis brjod cing/kun tu gnas nas smras pa de dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa la skur pa 'debs pa'o/
16a8(1): de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar de bzhin ni/g.yo' ba myed pa/brlom sems myed pa'o/'di ltar de bzhin nyid ni/nyan pa myed pa'o/'di ltar de bzhan nyid ni stong pa'o/mtshan ma myed pa'o/smond pa myed pa'o/
16a9(1): yang btsun ba sha ri'i bu gang 'das pa'i de bzhin nyid de yang dmyigs su myed/ma 'ongs pa'i de bzhin nyid de la dmyigs su myed/gang da ltar byung pa'i de bzhin nyid de yangadamyigs su myed pa nas de bzhin du sbyar te/sem
16a10(1): sa kyi bar de bzhin nyid de yang myigs su myed/'on kyang btsun ba sha ri'i bu/de bzhin nyid ma rtogs par/gang bstand pa 'am/rab tu bshad bar nus pa'i chos gzhan gang myi dmyigs so/sha ri'i bus smras pa/
16b1(1): 'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa/de bzhin nyid la bzhugs nas chos bstond tam //'jam dpal gyis smras pa/btsun ba sha ri'i bu //de bzhin nyid yod pa ma yin na/ci ltar de bzhin gshegs pa/
16b2(1): de bzhin nyid la zhugs te chos bstond do/btsun ba sha ri'i bu chos gang yang yod pa ma yin na/ciltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i de bzhin nyid la bzhugs te/chos bstond pa/de bzhin gsheg+sa pa yang yod pa ma yin naci lta
16b3(1): ra de bzhin gshegs pa de bzhin nyid la bzhugste/chos bstond /chos thams cad yod pa ma yin te/dmyigs su myed/de bzhin gshegs pa yang yod pa ma yin te dmyigs su myed do//de'i chos bstand pa ni/
16b4(1): 'di dra ste/'di la dmyigs pa 'am/myi dmyigs pa gnyis kyis rab tu phye ba myed do//de bzhin gshegs pa ni brjod pa 'am/myi brjod pas rab tu phye mA yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/btsun ba sha ri'i bu/'di ltar
16b5(1): de bzhin gshegs pa ni/brjod pa yang dag bar 'chad pa/brtag pa myed pa/gdags par bya ba ma yin pa'o/sha ri'i bus smras pa 'jam dpal gis/chos bstand pa 'di lta 'di'i snod du gyur ro/
16b6(1): 'das pa'i chos rnams myi dmyigs shing/'das pa'i chos rnams kun shesa bar myi 'gyur pa dang/gang 'das pa dang/ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar 'byung pa'i chos rnams myi dmyigsshing/'das pa dang
16b7(1): ma 'ongs pa dang/da lta 'byung ba'i chos rnams kun shes par myi 'gyur te/chos bstand pa 'di lha bu ni snod du 'gyur ro//gang kun nas nyon mongs pa dang/rnam par byang ba myi mthong zhing/de nye bar lend bar yang
16b8(1): myi 'gyur ba de/chos bstand pa 'di ltar bu snod du 'gyur te/de ni bshad pa'i don kun shes par 'gyur ro/sha ri'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal 'di la ci zhig kun shes par 'gyur/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/btsun ba sha
16b9(1): ri'i bu bshad pa 'di 'i don shes bar 'gyur pa ci zhig yod na/'di la ci zhig kun shes bar 'gyur zhes de skad 'dri/sha ri'i bus smras pa/'jam du dpal gzho nu/chos zab mo bstan pa 'di/mngon gsum du 'gyur pa nyung/
16b10(1): 'dir yongs su 'dzind par 'gyur ba nyung ngo/'jam dpal dgra bcom pa dang/slob pa dang /myi slob pa rnams kyang sa phyogs 'di nas/sgyid lug bar myi 'gyur/byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams lta ci smos/'jam dpal gyis smrasapa
17a1(1): @#///btsun ba sha ri'i bur bcom ba rnams la sa myed do/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar dgra bcom ba yang myed na/sa gang la dgra bcom ba gnas bar 'gyur/dgra bcom ba bdag ni gnas myed bas rab tu phye ba'o//
17a2(1): dgra bcom ba nyid ni dmyigs su myed bas rab tu phye pa'o//dgra bcom ba dag ni rjod pa dang/myi brjod pa shind tu chad bas rab tu phye ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ldar dgra bcom ba dag ni brjod pa dang/myi brjod payang dag bar mchad

17a8(1): myed pa'i tshul gyis dge' sbyong gi chos la gnas ba zhes bya'o//de nas pajam dpal zho nur gyurd bas bstand pa 'di bshad pa na 'khor de'i dge slong lnga rgya bo stand las langs te/'di skad ces/'jam dpal zho nur gyurang ba myi lta'o//'jam 
17a9(1): dpal zho nur gyurang pa myi gnyan to//'jam dpal zho nur gyur pa phyogs gang na gnas pa'i phyogs de yang spang bar bya'o/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar 'jam dpal zho nur gyuradapa ni/bstond pa mngon gsum du gyurd pa bzhin du/kun nas nyon mong
17a10(1): dam pa dang /rnam bar byang ba'i mtshan nyid gcig tu bstond bas chos ma yin ba smra ba'o/zhes brjod nas/de dag yang 'di snyam du sems te/bdag cag ci ltar bcom ldan 'das kyis/legs bar gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba la tshangs bar

17b5(1): 'jam dpal gzhonur gyurd pa phyogs gang na gnas pa'i phyogs de spang bar bya'o /de ci'i phyir zhe na /'jam dpal zho nur gyur pa ma yin te /dmyigs su myed na gang de'i gnas kyang yod pa ma yin te /dmyigs du myed do /

17b10(1): stond pa'i nyan thos shes bya /mchog shes ba zhes bya /pa /pa /po /zhes bya /sbyin gnas shes bya /bstan pa 'di bshad pa na /dge slong lnga brgya po de dag las dge slong bzhi brgya ni /lend pa myed bar zag pa las sems rnam bar grol lo //yang dag mtha' man bar grol lo //
18a1(1): /dge slong brgya ni sems cher 'khrugs nas/lus de nyid dang/sems can dmyal ba chen po (du)'bod du lhung ngo/de nas tshe dang ldan ba sha ri'i bus/'jam dpal zho nur gyurd pa la 'di skad ces smras so/'jam 
18a2(1): dpal khyod sems can yongs su/srung ba'i chos myi bstond bas /dge slong lnga brgya po de dag cung zos te mtshar byas so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis/gnas gtan sha ri'i bu la 'di skad ces bka' stsald to//sha ri'i bu khyod
18a3(1): de skad ma zer cig/de ci'i phyir zhe na/sha ri'i bu dge slong lnga brgya po/de dag sems can dmyal ba chen pir 'du 'bod du reg pa thang cig/reg nas dga' ldan gyi lha rnams dang skal ba mnyam bar skye bar 'gyur ro/sha ri'i bu gal

18a5(1): nas bskald par sems can dmyal ba myong bar 'gyur pa'i las de yad cung zad cig myong bar byas so//sha ri'i bu de'i phyir dge slong brgya po de dag de bzhin gshegs pa byams pa'i nyan thos thog ma dus du ma byas bar 'gra bcom

18a7(1): pa bzhi ni de lta ma yin/gzugs myed pa'i snyoms bar 'jug pa bzhi sgoms bas ni de lta ma yin no/de ci'i phyir zhe na/chos 'di lta bu ma thos bar 'khor ba las yongs su grol/skye ba dang /dga' ba dang/

22b4(1): bcom ldan 'das ga la ba dor dor ste /lhags nas/so so'i bya skad kyi rol mo'i sgra/dpe' myed pa'i mdangs kyis /sangs rgyas kyi yang dag pa'i bstod pa dag gis bcom ldan 'das la mngon bar stond nas/bcom 
22b5(1): ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgobos phyag 'tshal te//phyogs cig tu makhod do/bya'i rigs 'dres pa de dag thams cad kyang /byang chub sem/sa dpa' sems dpa' chenapo ting nge 'dzind tha dad pa la dag la rnam bar 
22b6(1): rtse ba sha stag go/yang de'i tshe seng ge'i rgyal po rin cen bad ma las stsogs pa /seng ge'i rgyal bo 'bum phrag grangs myed pa la/seng ge'i rgyal po re re yang 'khod sang nge grangs myed pa dag /
22b7(1): bcom ldan 'das ga la pa der dong ste lhags nas /seng ge'i sgra skad kyi rol mo'i sgra snyand bas/bcom ldan 'das (la)sangs rgyas kyi yon tan yang dag pa dag gyis yang dag bar bstond nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo
22b8(1): pos phyag 'tshal te/phyogs gcig tu mkhod do //seng ge'i rgyal po de dag thams cad kyang /byang (ba)chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po /ting nge 'dzind tha dad pa dag la rnam bar rtse sha stag go/yang de'i che/spos kyi 
22b9(1): glang po /ki ra'i nang du na lta bu /zhes bya ba las gsogs pa/spos kyi glang po'i rgyal po /'bum phrag grangs myed pa la /spos kyi glang po re re yang/'khord spos kyi glang po/'bum phrag grangs myed pa dang/bcom 
22b10(1): ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste lhags te/bcom ldan 'das la sangs rgyas kyi yon tan grangs myed pa dag gis bstod de/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhalas la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas lan gsum bskor ba byas nas/phyogs cig mkhod do/
23a1(1): @#//spos kyi glang po che de dag thams cad kyang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po /ting nge 'dzind tha dad pa dag la rnam par rtse ba sha stag go //yang de'i tshe/sprul dang /stag dang /ri dgas tha dad pa 'dres pa
23a2(1): gnas pa thams cad bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste/lhags nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo pos phyag 'tshal te/phyogs gcig tu mkhod do//sprul dang /stag dang/ri dgas de dag thams cad kyang/
23a3(1): theg pa chen po la mngond tu phyogs pa dang/theg pa chen po la yang dag par zhugs pa'i/byang chub sems dpa' sesam dpa' chen po/ting nge 'dzind tha dad de dag la/rnam par rtse ba sha stag go//yang de'i tshe/sha sprang dang/
23a4(1): sbrang bu pu ri sa dang/sbrang ma dang/bi shu ka dang/1 dang/rus spal dang/srin bu byis ba gsong dang/skye sar dang/'ug pa dang/sdig pa dang/srin gyi mying grangs myed pa/de dag dang/sdig sprul rigs 
23a5(1): tha dad pa/'dres pa/gnas pa thams cad dang/phag dang/khyi dang/zha dang/gzhan yang srog chags phra mo rnams dang/bcom ldan 'das ga la bder dong ste lhags nas/sangs rgyas kyi 
23a6(1): yon tan grangs myed pa dag gis bcom ldan 'das la mngon par bstod/de/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo pos phyag 'tshal te/phyogs cig tu mkhod do//yang de'i tshe/sbrul dang/myur du 'dug pa 
23a7(1): dang/sbrul gzi gu dang/chu sbrul myig ma zad bar 'dug pa'i rigs/'bum phrag grangs myed pa dag//bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste/lhags nas/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan grangs myed pa dgagasi/sangs 
23a8(1): rgyas bcom ldan 'das la bstod de/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhalas la mgo pos phyag 'tshalate/phyogs cig tu mkhod de/yang de'i tshe/lhun po dang/lhun po chen po dang/btang bzung dang/btang bzungs chen po dang/
23a9(1): 'khor yug dang/'khor yug chen po dag dang/ri gzhan dag ni lha rnams rang rang gi khang bar zhugs nas/bcom ldan 'das ga la bder dong ste lhags nas/bcom ldan 'da' la sangs rgyas kyi yon tan yang dag pa dag gis mngon
23b1(1): bar bstod de/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo pos phyag 'tshal te/phyogs cig tu mkhod do/lha de dag thamam cad kyang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po/ting nge 'dzind tha dad pa dag la rnam bar
23b2(1): rtse ba sha stag go/yang de'i tshe/sman dang /nags kyi lha thams cad rang rang gyi khang bar zhugs nas/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste/lhags nas/bcom ldan 'da' la sangs rgyas kyi yon tan yang dag pa dag gis bstod
23b3(1): de/bcom ldan 'das kyizhabs la mgo pos phyag 'atsal te/phyogs cig tu mchod do/lha de dag thams cad kyang /byang chub sesam dpa' sems dpa' chen po/ting nge 'dzind tha dang pa dag la/rnam bar rtse sha stag go/yang de'i
23b4(1): tshe/byang chub semam dpa' sems dpa' chenapo sprin myed cheno snying po 'khor rnams dang /byang chub sems dba' kha cig 'di snyam du sems te/bcom ldan 'das kyi skad cig cig la //bskad pa brgya tsho dpag tu myed pa/grangs 
23b5(1): myed pa/bsam gyis myi khyab pa/tshungs pa myed pa/gzhal du myed pa/tshad myed pa/brjod du myed pa/'di yang brjoddu myed pa /la drug bar mdzad pa de ni ci ltar shes bar rung /bcom lnga
23b6(1): 'das rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa'i/bskald pa bye ba phrag ci snyed/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gi khams/kun nas nyon mongs pa can dag tu ni thabs rnam pa sna tshogs la mkhas pa'i tshul gyi 'tshams/sbyor ba'i 
23b7(1): 'jig rten gyi spyod pa rnams dang/ye shes gzigs pa dang/khams dang /rgyu dang /dmyigs pa dang/nges pa'i tshig dang/gdags pa dang/rnam bar dag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyi /stobs la sbyor ba dag gis /sems can mtsho ci snyed
23b8(1): cig/bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yongs su smyind bar 'tshal ba bzhi gu snyam mo//yang de'i tshe/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po/sgrib pa thams cad rnam bar gseld pa/sangs rgyas kyis gnang ste/
23b9(1): myi 'jigs ba thob bas/byang chub sems dpa'-i sems dpa' chen po/de dag gi sems kyi yongs su rtogs pa/sems kyis shes nas /byang chub sems dpa'aM sems dpa' chen po/smin chen po la 'di skad ces smras so/sprin chen 
23b10(1): snying po /sprin chen po'i mdo la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi /dga' ldan gi gnas su skye ba/rnam bar gzhag pa'i byin kyis rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i /chos snang ba'i sgo yod do/sprin cen snying po/
24a1(1): @#///srin chen po'i mdo' la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi dga' ldan gi gnas su /skye ba rnam par bzhag pa'i/byin kyi rlabs su stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i stobs /snang ba'i sgo yod do/sprin chen 
24a2(1): snying po sprin chen snying po'i mdo' la /de bzhin gshegs pa rnam kyi dga' ldan gi gnas/'chi 'pho rnam par dag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya'i/chos snang ba'i sgo yod do//sprin cen snying po /sprin chen po'i 
24a3(1): mdo' la /de bzhin gsheg pa rnams kyi /lha mo sgyu 'phrul gi gnas su /cha phra bar snang la rnam par bzhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyi  stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya pa'i chos snang pa'i sgo yod do//sprin chen snying po /sprin chen bo'i mdo la /de ba 
24a4(1): bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi/lha mo sgyu 'phrul gi lhums su /cha phra bar snang pa rnam par bzhag pa'i byinakyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor pa zhes bya ba'i/chos la snang ba'i sgo yod do/spri chen snying po /sprin chem po'i 
24a5(1): mdo' la /de bzhIn gshegs pa rnams kyi yum ni skyed mo 'tshal du /sku ltam pa rnam par bzhag pa'i byan kyi rlabs kyi stobs la spyor ba zhes bya ba'i/chos snang ba'i sgo yod/sprin cen snying po /spri chen po'i
24a6(1): 'od las /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi yul ni skyed mo 'tshal du /zhugs pa rnam par dag pa'i/byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya
24a7(1): snying po'i mdo la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnam kyi yul ni skyed mo 'tshal tu /phyogs bcu gzigs pa rnam par dag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs la/sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i/chos kyi snang ba'i sgo yod do /sprin cen snying po /sprin chen
24a8(1): chen po'i mdo la /de bzhin gshegs pa dmas kyi yul ni skyed mo 'atsal du /gsung rjod pa rnam par bzhag pa'i/byin kyi rlabs kyis stobs la sbyor ba zhes ba'i/chos snang pa'i sgo yod do /sprin chen snying po /sprin chen po'i 
24a9(1): mdo la/de bzhin gshegasapa rnams kyi yul ni skyed ma 'tshal nas/grong khyer chen po ser ba spyi'i gnas su/de bzhin gshegs pa rnal bar bzhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyis stobs sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i/chos snang pa'i

24b6(1): mdo la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi btsun mo'i 'khor da na/bzhugs pa rnam bar dag pa'i/byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs las sbyor ba zhes bya ba 'i/chos snang ba'i yod do//sprin chen snying po/sprin chen
24b7(1): po ni mdo la//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kye skyed mo 'tshal gyi sar gshegs pa/rnam bar bzhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyi stonabs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'/chos snang ba'i sgo yod do//sprin chen snyid po/sprin chen po'i
24b8(1): mdo la/de gzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis rgan mo dang/nad pa dang/shi ba dang/rab du 'byung ba la gzigs pa/rnam bar bzhag ba'i byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs ma sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i/chosakyi snang ba'i sgo yod do/sprin cen nyid po/

24b10(1): snying po/sprin cen po'i mdo la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnamasakyi/na bza' dur myig bzhes pa rnam bar gzhag pa'i/byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor pa zhes bya ba'i/chos snang pa'i sgo yod do/sprin chen snying po/sprin chen po

25a2(1): la /de bzhin gshegs pa thams kyi dka' ba spyod pa las /bzhengs pa rnam bar bzhag pa'i byan kyi rlabs la sbyor pa zhes bya ba'i /chos snang ba'i sgo yod do//sprin cen snying po /spri chen po'i mdo la /de bzhin gshags pa

25a8(1): sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i /chos snang ba zhes bya ba'i sgo yod do /spri chen snying po /sprin chen pa'i mdo las /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi mngon bar rjogs bar byang chub pa/rnam bar gzhag pa'i byi rlabs kyi stobs kyi spyor ba zhes bya ba'i/
25a9(1): chos snang ba'i sgo yod do//sprin can snying po /sprin cen po'i mdo la /de bzhan gshegs pa rnams kyi chos kyi 'khor lo /bskor bar skul ba rnam bar gzhag pa //byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs las sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i //chos snang ba'i sgo yod
25a10(1): do/sprin chen snying po //sprin chen snying po'i mdo la /de bzhingshegs pa rnams kyi lan gsum du bsdus te/chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba rnam bar bzhag pa'i //byin kyi rlabs kyi stogs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i /chos snang pa'i sgo yod do /
25b1(1): sprin cen snying po /sprin chen po'i mdo la /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi mye'i 'od dang /glog gi 'od dang /zla ba dang /nyi ma'i 'od dang /nor bu'i 'od dang /brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang /'jig rten skyong kyi 'od /rnam bar gzhag pa'i byin 
25b2(1): kyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i /chos snang ba'i sgo yod do /sprin chen snying po /sprin chen pa'i mdo la /de bzhin gshes pa rnams kyi rgyal po'i bka' /rnam bar bzhag pa'i byin rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor pa zhes bya ba'i /chos
