25b4(1): sprin chen po'i mdo la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnam kyi lo ba dang /phye bo dang /lkug pa dang /'on ba rnam bar gzhag pa'i /byin kyi rlabs gyi stobs la /sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i /chosasnang ba'i sgo yod do /sbrin cen snying po /
25b5(1): sbrin chen po'i mdo la /de bzhig gshegs pa rnams kyi sems can dmyal dang /dud gro dang /yi dgas dang /shing rje'i 'jig rten du skye ba /rnam bar bzhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs las sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i /cho snang ba
25b6(1): 'i sgo yod do /sprin can snying po /sprin chen po'i mdo la /de bzhin gshegs ba rnams kyi myi khom ba rnam pa sna tshogs su /skye ba rnam bar bzhag pa'i /byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i /chos snang ba'i sgo yod do /sprin cen
25b7(1): snying po /sprin chen po'i mod la /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi dge ba cu'i las kyi lam ba rnam bar bzhag pa'i /byin kyi rlabasakyi stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i //chos snang ba'i sgo yodado /sprin cen snying po /sprin chen pa'i mdo la /
25b8(1): de bzhin gshegs pa rnamasakyi bsod nams bya ba'i dngos po /rnam pa sna tshogs rnam bar bzhag pa'i byin kyi rlabras kyi stobs la sbyor ba zhes ba ba'i /chos snang ba'i sgo yod do //sprin cen snying po /sprin chen po'i mdo la /de bzhin gshe
25b9(1): gas pa rnams kyi spyod pa dang /kun tu rgyu gcer bu las stsogs ba /mu stegs bye ba rnam bar bzhag ba'i byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor ba zhe sa bya ba'i /chos snang pa'i sgo yod do //sprin cen snying po /spri chen po'i mdo las /
26a1(1): @#//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi/dug rgya bcu rtsa bzhi kun tu 'khrug pa rnam par bzhag pa'i/byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i/chos snang ba'i sgo yod do/sprin cen snying po/sprin chen po'i mdo la/de bzhin 
26a2(1): gshegs pa rnams kyi/'phrog ma kun tu 'khrug pa'i/rnam par gzhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyar ba zhes bya ba'i/chos snang ba'i sgo yod do//sprin chen snying po/sprin chen bo'i mdo la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnamasakyi/
26a3(1): sor mo'i 'phreng ba kun tu 'khrug pa/rnam par bzhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyistobs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i/chos snang pa'i sgo yod do/sprin ces snying po/sprin chen po' mdo la/de bzhin gshes pa rnams kyi/gnod sbyin gyi 'phrog gnas/
26a4(1): kun tu 'khrug pa rnam parabazhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs ki stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya pa'i/chos snang pa'i sgo yong ngo /sprin cen snying po//sprin chen po'i mdo la/de bzhin gshegs pa dmas kyi/klu'i rgyal po gsog ma myed/
26a5(1): rnam par bzhag pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i//chos snang ba'i sgo yod do/sprin can snying po/sprin chen po'i mdo la/de bzhin gshigs pa rnams kyi chos dkun tu 'khrug pa'i/
26a6(1): rnam par bzhag pa'i bin kyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor par byed pa zhes bya ba'i/chos snang ba'i sgo yod do//sprin cen snying po/sprin chen bo'i mdo la/de bzhin gshegs parnams kyi myi ngas kun tu 'khrug pa'i/rnam par
26a7(1): bzhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs la spyor ba zhes bya ba'i/chos snang pa'i sgo yod do//sprin cen snying po /srin chen po'i mdo la//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi klu'i gnas dag tu/bye ma rab tu 'bar pa/
26a8(1): kun tu 'khrug pa rnam par gzhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyistobs la sbyor ba zhes bya pa'i/chos snang ba'i sgo yong ngo /srin cen snying po/srin chen po'i mdo la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi klu'i gnas dag tu/nam mkha' 
26a9(1): ldir kun tu 'khrug pa rnam paragazhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs pa/stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i//sprin chen snying po/sprin chen po'i mdo la/de bzhin gshags pa rnams kyi brag byed ba dang/'jog po kun tu 'khrug pa rnam
26b1(1): bar gzhag pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyi stobs la sbyor ba zhes bya ba'i/chos snang pa'i sgo yod do/sprin cen snying bo/sprin chen po'i mdo la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi glang po che nor gyis 'jug kun tu/'khrug pa'i rnam
26b2(1): bar gzhag pa'i byin kyi rlab kyi stobs la sbyor ba zhes ba bya ba'i chos snang pa'i sgo yod do//sprin chen snying po/sprin chen po'i mdo la /de bzhin gshegs pa rnam kyi/bram ze'i bu mo/blu byed kun tu 'khrug pa/rnam bar bzhag pa'i byin

56b6(1): bdag myed pa mos pa dang/bdag sdong bas chos thams cad sdong bar som nyid myed pa dang/bdag dben pas/chos thams cad dben pa la 'jug pa dang/bdag zhi bas chos thams cad zhi bar so sor rtog pa dang/bdag ji lta
56b7(1): ba bzhin bas/chos thams cad ci lta ba bzhin du khong du chud pa dang/bdag zab pas/chos thams cad zab par nges par sems pa/dang/bdag bems po yin pas/chos thams cad bems po yin

56b10(1): pa dang/phyind ci log tu shes pas chos thams cad smyig rgyu lta bur rab tu shes bas/rgyu'i bya ba dang/rkyen la dmyigs te/skye bas chos thams cad myig yor lta bur rab tu shes pa dan/'pho sha myed pa'i mtshan nyid
56b11(1): las phyir ma log pas/chos thams cad chu zla lta bur rab tu shes pa dag/yul na myi gnas shing/phyogs na myi gnas te/ngo bo nyid myed pas/chos thams can brag cha lta bur rab tu shes pa da/skad cig kyang myi sdod pas

59b7(1): kun tu yongs su sngo ba shes pa la mkhas pa dang/so so yang dag bar rig pa thob pa yin te /don dang/chos dang /nges pa'i tshig dang /spo bas pa la mkhas pa dang /gzugs thams cad

59b10(1): skad smras pa de bzhin du byed (pa)dang/bdag gi 'khruld pa la so sor rtog bang byed kyi /gzhan gi 'khrul pa la myi gartog pa dang/srog gi phyir yang gzhan dag la (I)rnyas bar myi byed pa dang/rnyed pa dang bkur sti thob bas kyang 
60a1(1): @#////thob bar myi 'dzind pa dang /sems can thams cad la yon gnas kyi sems skyed de /sbyin ba sbyin ba dang /bgro bsha'i ngang tshul can yin te/chos la slob dpon gi dpe' 'khrud myi byed pa dang /gzhan gyi
60a2(1): bde bas dga' ba yin te/phrag dog dang/ser sna dang bral ba dang/sdug pa dang /myi sdug pa thams cad yongasu skyong ba la 'gyod pa myed pa ste /klu'i bdag po chos brgyad po chos de dag dang ldan na/byang chub sems dpa' phyir myi
60a3(1): ldog pa'i chos la/yang dag bar gzhol ba yin no//klu'i bdag po chos de dag dang ldan /byang chub sems dpa' bla na myed pa yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i byang chub las phyir myi ltog pa yin te/lnga gang zhe na/pha rold tu phyind pa thams cad yong

60a9(1): ba'i chos rnam bar dag ba'i phyir/legs bar gnas pa'i chos nyid de/gsum po de dag go /klu'i rgyal po gzhan yang chos gsum dang ldan na /byang chub sems dpa' bzod thob pa yin na/gsum gang zhe na/lus yongs su dag
60a10(1): bar bya ba'i phyir/bsod nams kyis nye bar bstand pa dang/dag yongs su dag bar bya ba' phyir/ye shes mtha' yas pa dang/sems can yongs su dag bar bya ba'i phyir /ting nge 'dzind la dmyigs pa ste/klu'i bdag po chos gsum po

64b9(1): 'das kyis bka' bstsald pa/klu'i bdag po de bas ni/gang gzugs myed cing stan tu myed pa/bzung du myed pa yi ge myed pa/brjod du myed pa'i chos 'di/sems can gzhan rnams dang/gang zag gzhan rnams
64b10(1): dang/brda sprod cing yi ge rnams kyis mtshon te/thabs sna tshogs kyis go bar byas pa de/de bzhin gshegs pas ches dga' ba mdzad pa yin no//klu'i bdag po de bas kyang/gang dag 'di ltar bstand pa'i/
65a1(1): @#////chos 'di dag la mos bar 'gyur pa 'am /'jug bar 'gyur ba de dag ni ches ngo tshard che ba byed bar bya'o/klu'i bdag po gzhan yang /zab mo'i tshul 'di la 'jug pa'i sems can gang yin ba de dag ni /sngon gi 
65a2(1): de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la yongs su sbyar bar byas pa yin te/de dag la ni bdud sdig can gis kyang rlags ba myi snyed do/klu'i bdag po bdag gyis mngon bar shes pa 'das pa'i dus na /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das tshad myed pa dang
65a3(1): mnyes bar byas te/mchod bar yang byas /de dag gi bstand pa la rab tu 'byung ste/tshangs bar spyod pa yang spyad na /'on kyang de bzhin gshesga pa de dag gis nga la /'di ltar zab cing 'di ltar rgyu ba chad pa'i chos ma bstand te/gzhan du
65a4(1): na sbyong ba rta ma nyi tshe dang/sbyin ba dang/dul ba dang /legs bar sdom ba dang/nges pa'i rta ma nyi tshe dang /tshul khrims dang/thos pa dang/gtong ba dang /bzod pa dang/brtson 'grus dang/bag yod pa'i gtam nyi tse/

65a7(1): bas na rnam grangs 'dis kyang khyod kyis 'di ltar zab cing /nga'o snyam ba'i nga rgyal las rnam bar grol ba/yongs su rtogs bar byas pa /rgyud dang/rkyen la rag las pa/sems can dang/srog dang/gso' ba dang/gang zag

68a10(1): pa'i gtam zad pa 'am //pha rold tu phyind pa'i gtam zad pa myed do /klu'i bdag po 'di ltar na /yi ge thams cad bstand pa la zad pa myed pa gang yin pa 'di ni /myi zad pa'i za' ma tog ces bya ba'i
68b1(1): gzungs shes bya'o//klu'i bdag po myi zad pa'i za' ma tog gi gzungs 'di ni /myi zad pa nyid rtogs bar bya ste/bzhi gang zhe na /so so yang dag bar rig pa myi zad pa nyid dang/ye shes myi zad pa nyid dang/
68b2(1): shes rab myi zad pa nyid dang/gzugs kyi sbobs pa myi zad pa nyid dang /bzhi po de dag go /klu'i bdag po myi zad pa'i za' ma tog gi gzung 'di/gting dpag dka' ba bzhis rtogs bar bya ste/bzhi gang zhe na

68b9(1): gi gzungs kyis /chos tshol ba la thard thug pa myed pa ste /bzhi gang zhe na /pha rold tu phyind pha yongs su tshol ba thar phyind pa myed ba ste /bzhi bo de dag go/klu'i bdagabo bzhi bo 'di dag ni /myi zad ba'i
